Surah: 73. Follow the brief below (surah_map.md) exactly. The evidence is text.md (the surah), dictionary.md (every root of the surah's words, each branch with the classical dictionaries' own phrases), channels.md and hft.md (both are earlier readers' proposals: ignore their judgements (grades, strength or confidence labels, reading types, statements of what a reading may or may not do) and make your own) and your own knowledge of Arabic and the Quran. Return only the map as your final message.

===== _commentary/v16/prompts/r2/surah_map.md =====
Read the surah as one text and write its map of image chains: the lexical images that run through several of
its ayat and join them into one scene, process or movement. A later writer will read one ayah at a time, with
only that ayah's own dictionary. The map lets that writer hear what the ayah's words carry in the surah as a
whole, including senses whose evidence sits under the words of other ayat.

Your evidence is the surah text, the dictionary of every root in the surah (each branch with the classical
dictionaries' own phrases), earlier readers' channel review (channels.md) and activation hypotheses (hft.md),
and your own knowledge of Arabic and the Quran. Where a member or passage comes from memory rather than from
the dictionary or the text, say so. Do not use tools, delegate, browse or inspect files.

The channel review and the HFT records are proposals by earlier readers. Ignore their judgements: grades,
strength or confidence labels, reading types, words such as "surprising", "exploratory" or "latent", and every
statement of what a reading may or may not do. Make your own judgement from the surah's words, the dictionary
phrases and the Quran. Do not rediscover what they already assembled: start from their chains, test each one
against the dictionary phrases and the text, and join, extend, split or correct them. Where their wording
abstracts a member, go back to the dictionary phrase and name what it actually says.

A chain belongs on the map when its members are senses the dictionary attests for words that stand in the
surah, and together they make one image or process that the surah's wording or sequence lets a listener hear.
A member need not be the sense that translates its word in its own ayah; a chain is heard across the surah,
not in one word. Say plainly when a member is a rare sense or is attested only inside a fixed expression, and
keep it. When the dictionary itself joins two of the surah's words in one phrase, quote that phrase: it is
the strongest evidence a chain can have. Keep a scene at the level of its objects, their parts and their
operation. When proposals share members, do not fold one into another's more abstract function unless nothing
concrete is lost. Carry every chain that meets this test, however unusual; leave out proposals that do not.

Write the map in English, with Arabic quoted exactly (surah wording from the text, dictionary phrases from the
dictionary). It is working material for the writer, not commentary prose.

1. `## Chains`. For each chain, a `###` heading naming the image. One paragraph on what the image is and how it
   moves through the surah. Then its members, one line each: the ayah ref, the word as it stands in the text,
   the root and branch id, the dictionary's own phrase quoted exactly, and what this member contributes to the
   image. Add Quran passages outside the surah, with exact refs, where they stage or confirm the chain.
2. `## Ayat`. For each ayah in order: which chains it opens, advances or completes, and what its words add to
   each.
3. `## Not carried`. Each channel subchannel and each HFT record you did not carry into a chain, one line each:
   its name and why.

No ranking and no labels of strength or confidence. No list of what a writer must include. There is no length
target and no required number of chains or members.


===== _commentary/v16/work/s073/surah.r2/text.md =====
# Surah 73

- 73:1 يَٰٓأَيُّهَا ٱلْمُزَّمِّلُ
- 73:2 قُمِ ٱلَّيْلَ إِلَّا قَلِيلًۭا
- 73:3 نِّصْفَهُۥٓ أَوِ ٱنقُصْ مِنْهُ قَلِيلًا
- 73:4 أَوْ زِدْ عَلَيْهِ وَرَتِّلِ ٱلْقُرْءَانَ تَرْتِيلًا
- 73:5 إِنَّا سَنُلْقِى عَلَيْكَ قَوْلًۭا ثَقِيلًا
- 73:6 إِنَّ نَاشِئَةَ ٱلَّيْلِ هِىَ أَشَدُّ وَطْـًۭٔا وَأَقْوَمُ قِيلًا
- 73:7 إِنَّ لَكَ فِى ٱلنَّهَارِ سَبْحًۭا طَوِيلًۭا
- 73:8 وَٱذْكُرِ ٱسْمَ رَبِّكَ وَتَبَتَّلْ إِلَيْهِ تَبْتِيلًۭا
- 73:9 رَّبُّ ٱلْمَشْرِقِ وَٱلْمَغْرِبِ لَآ إِلَٰهَ إِلَّا هُوَ فَٱتَّخِذْهُ وَكِيلًۭا
- 73:10 وَٱصْبِرْ عَلَىٰ مَا يَقُولُونَ وَٱهْجُرْهُمْ هَجْرًۭا جَمِيلًۭا
- 73:11 وَذَرْنِى وَٱلْمُكَذِّبِينَ أُو۟لِى ٱلنَّعْمَةِ وَمَهِّلْهُمْ قَلِيلًا
- 73:12 إِنَّ لَدَيْنَآ أَنكَالًۭا وَجَحِيمًۭا
- 73:13 وَطَعَامًۭا ذَا غُصَّةٍۢ وَعَذَابًا أَلِيمًۭا
- 73:14 يَوْمَ تَرْجُفُ ٱلْأَرْضُ وَٱلْجِبَالُ وَكَانَتِ ٱلْجِبَالُ كَثِيبًۭا مَّهِيلًا
- 73:15 إِنَّآ أَرْسَلْنَآ إِلَيْكُمْ رَسُولًۭا شَٰهِدًا عَلَيْكُمْ كَمَآ أَرْسَلْنَآ إِلَىٰ فِرْعَوْنَ رَسُولًۭا
- 73:16 فَعَصَىٰ فِرْعَوْنُ ٱلرَّسُولَ فَأَخَذْنَٰهُ أَخْذًۭا وَبِيلًۭا
- 73:17 فَكَيْفَ تَتَّقُونَ إِن كَفَرْتُمْ يَوْمًۭا يَجْعَلُ ٱلْوِلْدَٰنَ شِيبًا
- 73:18 ٱلسَّمَآءُ مُنفَطِرٌۢ بِهِۦ ۚ كَانَ وَعْدُهُۥ مَفْعُولًا
- 73:19 إِنَّ هَٰذِهِۦ تَذْكِرَةٌۭ ۖ فَمَن شَآءَ ٱتَّخَذَ إِلَىٰ رَبِّهِۦ سَبِيلًا
- 73:20 ۞ إِنَّ رَبَّكَ يَعْلَمُ أَنَّكَ تَقُومُ أَدْنَىٰ مِن ثُلُثَىِ ٱلَّيْلِ وَنِصْفَهُۥ وَثُلُثَهُۥ وَطَآئِفَةٌۭ مِّنَ ٱلَّذِينَ مَعَكَ ۚ وَٱللَّهُ يُقَدِّرُ ٱلَّيْلَ وَٱلنَّهَارَ ۚ عَلِمَ أَن لَّن تُحْصُوهُ فَتَابَ عَلَيْكُمْ ۖ فَٱقْرَءُوا۟ مَا تَيَسَّرَ مِنَ ٱلْقُرْءَانِ ۚ عَلِمَ أَن سَيَكُونُ مِنكُم مَّرْضَىٰ ۙ وَءَاخَرُونَ يَضْرِبُونَ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ يَبْتَغُونَ مِن فَضْلِ ٱللَّهِ ۙ وَءَاخَرُونَ يُقَٰتِلُونَ فِى سَبِيلِ ٱللَّهِ ۖ فَٱقْرَءُوا۟ مَا تَيَسَّرَ مِنْهُ ۚ وَأَقِيمُوا۟ ٱلصَّلَوٰةَ وَءَاتُوا۟ ٱلزَّكَوٰةَ وَأَقْرِضُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ قَرْضًا حَسَنًۭا ۚ وَمَا تُقَدِّمُوا۟ لِأَنفُسِكُم مِّنْ خَيْرٍۢ تَجِدُوهُ عِندَ ٱللَّهِ هُوَ خَيْرًۭا وَأَعْظَمَ أَجْرًۭا ۚ وَٱسْتَغْفِرُوا۟ ٱللَّهَ ۖ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ غَفُورٌۭ رَّحِيمٌۢ


===== _commentary/v16/work/s073/surah.r2/dictionary.md =====
# Dictionary: every branch of every focus root

Every root of the surah's words, each once, headed by the ayah and word where it first occurs; it also serves the later words listed with it.
One entry per branch: a Turkish label and Turkish glosses of its attested senses, then the classical dictionaries' own phrases with their source tags (ayn, sihah, tahdhib, maqayis, mufradat, jamhara). [kalıp] marks a sense the dictionaries attest only inside a fixed expression.
Identity roots come from the quran-data gateway. Word-scoped alternatives are cited analyses of this
exact word. **Echo roots** are observed but withheld mappings: sound-family candidates, not identity.

## ز م ل (root_000644): 73:1 ٱلْمُزَّمِّلُ

- **B001** yük, yük hayvanı ve tümünü birden taşıma — eşya veya yiyecek taşıyan yük devesi · bir şeyi bütünüyle tek seferde yüklenip taşımak · yük; taşınan şey · çok kalabalık bakmakla yükümlü kişiler · ailesi, hayvanları ve malıyla birlikte çıkmak ya da bunları geride bırakmak
  الزاملة بعير يحمل عليه متاعه (maqayis); الزاملة البعير يحمل عليه الطعام والمتاع (ayn;sihah); الازدمال احتمال الشيء كله بمرة واحدة (ayn;tahdhib); الزمل عند العرب الحمل (tahdhib); عيالات أزملة أي كثيرة (maqayis;sihah); خرج بأزملة إذا خرج بأهله وإبله وغنمه (tahdhib)

  المزاملة المعادلة على البعير (maqayis); الزميل الرديف على البعير والدابة (ayn;tahdhib); الزميل الرديف (sihah); زملت الرجل على البعير فهو زميل ومزمول إذا أردفته وزاملته عادلته (tahdhib)

  الزميل الرجل الضعيف (maqayis); الزميل الرذل من الرجال والزميلة والزمال (ayn); الزمل والزميل والزمال بمعنى الجبان الضعيف والزميلة الضعيفة (sihah); رجل زمال وزميلة وزميل إذا كان ضعيفا فسلا وهو الزمل أيضا (tahdhib); الزميل الضعيف (mufradat)
- **B005** ön ayaklara yüklenerek canlı ve hızlı ilerleme — hayvanın ön ayaklarına yüklenerek canlı biçimde koşması veya yürümesi · koşarken veya yürürken ön ayaklarından birine yüklenen · koşuya toparlanıp hızlanan at veya dağ keçisi
  الدابة تزمل في عدوها ومشيها زمالا إذا تتحامل على يديها بغيا ونشاطا (ayn;tahdhib); لاحق البطن إذا يعدو زمل (tahdhib); فرس أزمولة إذا تشمر في عدوه وأسرع (tahdhib); يقال للوعل أيضا أزمولة من سرعته (tahdhib)
- **B006** ses ve ses çıkaran hayvan — ses · ses çıkaran dağ keçisi veya başka bir hayvan

  هو ابن زوملتها أي عالمها (tahdhib); ابن زوملة أيضا ابن الأمة (tahdhib)

  قيم القوم من يسوس أمرهم ويقومهم؛ القائم في الملك ونحوه الحافظ؛ القيوم (ayn)؛ قوام أهل بيته وقيام أهل بيته؛ الذي يقيم شأنهم؛ القيوم اسم من أسماء الله (sihah)؛ قيم القوم الذي يقومهم ويسوس أمرهم؛ القائم بالأمر؛ القيوم القائم على كل شيء (tahdhib)؛ قيام للشيء هو المراعاة للشيء والحفظ له؛ قوامين لله؛ القيوم القائم الحافظ لكل شيء (mufradat)؛ قام بهذا الأمر إذا اعتنقه؛ قوام الدين والحق أي به يقوم (maqayis)

- **B007** başkasının yerini ve işlevini alma [kalıp] — onun yerine geçti veya adına görev yaptı

- **B008** düzgünlük, denge ve doğru yoldan sapmama — düzgün ve dengeli olmak; doğru yoldan ayrılmamak · düzgün, dengeli ve doğru
  رمح قويم ورجل قويم؛ القيمة الملة المستقيمة؛ إذا انقاد واستمرت طريقته فقد استقام (ayn)؛ الاستقامة الاعتدال؛ استقام له الأمر؛ قومت الشيء فهو قويم أي مستقيم؛ القوام العدل؛ دينا قيما (sihah)؛ الاستقامة على الطاعة؛ القيم هو المستقيم؛ أقوم كلاما أي أعدل كلاما (tahdhib)؛ الاستقامة في الطريق الذي يكون على خط مستو؛ استقامة الإنسان لزومه المنهج المستقيم؛ دينا قيما أي ثابتا (mufradat)
- **B009** ayakta tutan dayanak ve geçim temeli — bir şeyi ayakta tutan dayanak, düzen ve geçim temeli

- **B010** değer biçme ve belirlenen bedel — değer biçmeyle belirlenen bedel · malın değerini belirlemek veya ulaştığı bedeli bildirmek

  شددت العقد شدا (maqayis)؛ شد الحبل أو غيره (jamhara)؛ شده أي أوثقه (sihah)؛ شددت الشيء إذا أوثقته (tahdhib)؛ الشد العقد القوي وقويت عقده (mufradat)؛ شد عضده أي قواه (sihah)؛ شد الله ملكه وشدده أي قواه (sihah)؛ اشدد به أزرى (tahdhib)
- **B002** güç, katılık ve çetinlik — güç, katılık, dayanıklılık ve çetinlik · güçlü ve yürekli · ağır sıkıntı, çetin sınanma · büyük sarsıntılar ve ağır sıkıntılar · artırma ve ağırlaştırma · bir konuda çok sıkı davranma · hiçbir şeye gücü yetmemek · şarkı söylerken sesini yükseltmek için var gücünü kullanmak · güçlü bir bineğe sahip olmak · güçlü ve sert
  أصل واحد يدل على قوة في الشيء (maqayis)؛ الشدة الصلابة والنجدة وثبات القلب والمجاعة (ayn;tahdhib)؛ الشدة القوة في الجسم وصعوبة الزمن (jamhara)؛ الشدة القوة والجلادة والشديد الرجل القوي (tahdhib)؛ الشدة تستعمل في العقد وفي البدن وفي قوى النفس وفي العذاب (mufradat)؛ أصابتني شدى أي شدة (maqayis;sihah;tahdhib)؛ التشديد خلاف التخفيف (sihah)؛ ما أملك شدا ولا إرخاء لا أقدر على شيء (tahdhib)؛ تشددت القينة إذا جهدت نفسها (tahdhib)؛ كانت دوابهم شدادا وأشد الرجل إذا كانت معه دابة شديدة (maqayis;sihah)
- **B003** saldırıya atılma ve hızla koşma — düşmanın üzerine saldırmak · koşma, hızlı koşu · koşmak, hızla ilerlemek · tek bir saldırı hamlesi
  في الحرب أيضا يشد شدا (maqayis)؛ الشد الحمل وشد عليه في القتال (ayn;tahdhib)؛ شد على العدو إذا حمل عليه (jamhara;sihah)؛ الشد العدو والفعل اشتد (ayn;sihah)؛ الشد الحضر والفعل اشتد (tahdhib)؛ شد فلان على العدو شدة واحدة وشد شدات كثيرة (tahdhib)
- **B004** güç ve sağduyu bakımından olgunluğa erişme — güç, sağduyu ve deneyimin olgunluk düzeyi
  الأشد العشرون ويقال أربعون سنة (maqayis)؛ الأشد مبلغ الرجل الحنكة والمعرفة (ayn;tahdhib)؛ بلغ الرجل أشده والواحد شد (jamhara)؛ حتى يبلغ أشده أي قوته (sihah)؛ معناه الإدراك والبلوغ وأن يؤنس منه الرشد مع أن يكون بالغا (tahdhib)؛ يجتمع أمره وقوته ويكتهل وينتهي شبابه (tahdhib)
- **B005** günün ilerleyip yükselmesi [kalıp] — günün ilerleyip yükselmesi
  شد النهار ارتفاعه (maqayis;sihah)
- **B006** eli sıkılık — eli sıkı · eli sıkı
  الشديد والمتشدد البخيل (maqayis)؛ المتشدد البخيل (sihah)؛ لشديد أي لبخيل (tahdhib)

## و ط ء (root_001659): 73:6 وَطْـًٔا

- **B001** ayağıyla basma veya çiğneme — ayağıyla basmak veya çiğnemek · basılan yer; ayak basma yeri · yolu ayaklarıyla aşan yolcular · birine ya da bir hayvana bir şeyi bastırmak
  ووطئته برجلي أطؤه (maqayis)؛ الوطء بالقدم والقوائم (ayn;tahdhib)؛ ووطىء الأرض يطأها وطأ والموضع الموطىء (jamhara)؛ وطئت الشيء برجلي وطأ وهذا موطئ قدمك (sihah)؛ وطأته برجلي أطؤه وطأ ووطاءة (mufradat)
- **B002** hazırlayıp yumuşak, kolay ve rahat kılma — onun için yeri, işi, döşeği veya oturma yerini hazırlamak · kolay, yumuşak ve rahat · yumuşaklık ve kolaylık · altına serilen döşek · bineği rahat bulmak · konuk ağırlayan, yumuşak huylu ve cömert kişi
  كلمة تدل على تمهيد شيء وتسهيله (maqayis)؛ ووطأت لك الأمر ووطأت لك الفراش (ayn;tahdhib)؛ مكان وطيء بين الوطاءة (jamhara)؛ صار وطيئا واستوطأ المركب (sihah)؛ الوطيء من كل شيء ما سهل ولان (ayn;tahdhib)؛ الوطاء ما توطأت به ووطأت له بفراشه (mufradat)
- **B003** sertçe yakalayıp ezici baskıyla boyun eğdirme — sert yakalama, ezici baskı veya ağır saldırı · düşmanı ezici bir saldırıya uğratmak · belirtilen topluluğa yönelik baskıyı ağırlaştırma yakarışı
  وطئنا العدو وطأة شديدة (ayn;tahdhib)؛ الوطأة الأخذة (ayn;tahdhib)؛ الوطأة كالضغطة (sihah)؛ اشدد وطأتك على مضر (maqayis;ayn;sihah;tahdhib;mufradat)؛ أي ذللهم (mufradat)
- **B004** aynı konuda birbirine uyma ve uyuşma — bir işte birbirine uyma · bir iş üzerinde onunla anlaşmak · bir iş üzerinde birbirleriyle anlaşmak · bir sayıyı öteki sayıya uydurmak · okuyuşta işitme, görme, dil ve iç kavrayışın birbirine uyması
  المواطأة الموافقة على أمر (maqayis)؛ أوطأت فلانا وتواطأنا أي اتفقنا على أمر (ayn)؛ واطأته على الأمر مواطأة إذا وافقته (sihah)؛ واطأت فلانا وتواطأنا أي اتفقنا على أمر (tahdhib)؛ المواطأة الموافقة وأصله أن يطأ الرجل برجله موطئ صاحبه (mufradat)؛ ليواطئوا عدة ما حرم الله (sihah;mufradat)؛ وطاء بالمد أي مواطأة السمع والبصر وسمعه يواطىء قلبه (sihah;tahdhib)
- **B005** uyakta aynı sözcüğü aynı anlamla yineleme — şiirde aynı uyak sözcüğünü yeniden kullanma
  الإيطاء في الشعر اتفاق قافيتين على كلمة واحدة (ayn)؛ إعادة القافية (sihah;tahdhib)؛ اتفقت له قافيتان على كلمة واحدة معناهما واحد (tahdhib)
- **B006** kadınla cinsel birleşmeyi örtülü biçimde anlatma [kalıp] — kadınla cinsel birleşmede bulunmak; örtülü anlatım
  وطئت الجارية أي جامعتها (ayn;tahdhib)؛ ووطئ الرجل المرأة كناية عن النكاح (jamhara)؛ وطئ الرجل امرأته (sihah)؛ وطئ امرأته كناية عن الجماع (mufradat)
- **B007** biçime bağlı adlandırmalar — hurmanın süt, yağ veya kurutulmuş süt ürünüyle karıştırıldığı yiyecek · büyükçe çuval
  الوطيئة تمر يخرج نواه ويعجن باللبن (jamhara)؛ الوطيئة شيء كالغرارة وضرب من الطعام (sihah)؛ الوطيئة طعام للعرب تتخذ من التمر والغرارة أيضا (tahdhib)
- **B008** daha sağlam ve ağır duruş, daha açık söyleyiş — duruş bakımından daha sağlam ve ağır; söyleyiş bakımından daha açık
  قرئ أشد وطئا أي قياما (sihah)؛ هي أثبت قياما وأشد على المصلي (tahdhib)؛ من قرأ وطأ فمعناه هي أبلغ في القيام وأبين في القول (tahdhib)

## ن ه ر (root_001559): 73:7 ٱلنَّهَارِ, 73:20 وَٱلنَّهَارَ

- **B001** bol su taşıyan doğal akarsu yatağı — taşkın suyun aktığı doğal akarsu yatağı · akarsu yatakları · akarsular veya akarsu yatakları · akarsu yatağı sağlam bir güzergah edindi · su aktı ve kendine bir yatak açtı · bol sulu veya geniş akarsu · suyun kazıp açtığı yatak yeri · kuyu kazısı suya ulaştı
  النهر مجرى الماء الفائض (mufradat)؛ النهر واحد الأنهار وجمعه أنهار ونهر (ayn;sihah;tahdhib;maqayis)؛ سمي النهر لأنه ينهر الأرض أي يشقها (maqayis)؛ استنهر النهر أخذ مجراه (ayn;tahdhib;maqayis)؛ نهر الماء أو أنهر الماء جرى (sihah;tahdhib;maqayis)؛ نهر نهر كثير الماء (sihah;tahdhib;maqayis;mufradat)؛ حفرت البئر حتى نهرت أي بلغت الماء (tahdhib)
- **B002** şafaktan gün batımına aydınlık gündüz — şafaktan güneş batımına kadar süren aydınlık gündüz · aydınlık gündüz süreleri · gündüz vaktinde baskın yapan kimse · gündüzün aydınlığına girdik
  النهار ضياء ما بين طلوع الفجر إلى غروب الشمس (ayn;tahdhib;maqayis)؛ النهار ضد الليل (sihah)؛ الوقت الذي ينتشر فيه الضوء (mufradat)؛ النهار اسم لكل يوم (tahdhib)؛ النهار يجمع على نهر (sihah;tahdhib;maqayis)؛ رجل نهر صاحب نهار (ayn;sihah;tahdhib;maqayis;mufradat)
- **B003** bir şeyi açma veya genişletme — genişlik veya aydınlıkla birlikte genişlik · kanı açıp serbest bırakarak akıttı · yaranın veya yarığın açıklığını genişletti · genişledi · evlerin önleri arasında atık bırakılan açık alan · bağırsağı çözüldü ve akarsu gibi boşaldı · tehlikeler; birleşik bir türetme olarak açıklanan biçim
  أصل صحيح يدل على تفتح شيء أو فتحه (maqayis)؛ أنهرت الدم فتحته وأرسلته (maqayis)؛ أنهرت الدم أي أسلته (sihah;mufradat)؛ أنهرت الطعنة وسعتها وأنهر فتقها (sihah;tahdhib)؛ نهر من نهر الفتق (maqayis)؛ استنهر الشيء اتسع (sihah)؛ المنهرة فضاء يكون بين أفنية القوم (maqayis;sihah;mufradat)؛ أنهر بطنه إذا جاء بطنه مثل مجيء النهر (tahdhib)؛ النهر السعة تشبيها بنهر الماء وفي ضياء وسعة (mufradat;sihah;tahdhib)
- **B004** sert sözle azarlayıp engelleme [kalıp] — onu sert sözle azarlayıp engelledi
  نهرت الرجل نهرا وانتهرته انتهارا زجرته بكلام عن شر (ayn)؛ نهره وانتهره أي زبره (sihah)؛ نهرته وانتهرته إذا استقبلته بكلام تزجره (tahdhib)؛ النهر والانتهار الزجر بمغالظة (mufradat)
- **B005** bazı kuşların yavrusu — türü aktarıma göre değişen bir kuş yavrusu
  النهار فرخ القطا والغطاط والعقاب ونحوه وثلاثة أنهرة (ayn)؛ النهار فرخ الحبارى (sihah;tahdhib;mufradat)؛ النهار فرخ بعض الطير مما لا يعرج على مثله ولا معنى له (maqayis)
- **B006** fırsat kollayıp gizlice kapma — fırsat kollayıp ani biçimde gizlice kapma
  النهر الدغرة وهي الخلسة (tahdhib)
- **B007** kişi, yer ve yıldızlara ait özel adlar — belirli bir topluluktan bir şairin adı · bir yer adı · sularının bolluğu nedeniyle iki yıldız için kullanılan ortak ad
  نهار بن توسعة اسم شاعر من تميم (sihah)؛ نهروان بلد (sihah)؛ العرب تسمي العواء والسماك الأنهرين لكثرة مائهما (tahdhib)
- **B008** bulut — bulut
  الناهُور السحاب (tahdhib)

## س ب ح (root_000666): 73:7 سَبْحًا

- **B001** Tanrı'yı yücelterek anma ve kulluk — dua ve anma biçimindeki gönüllü kulluk · Tanrı'yı sözle, eylemle veya niyetle yüceltme ve anma
  السُّبحة وهي الصلاة (maqayis)؛ التسبيح يكون في معنى الصلاة (ayn)؛ سبح الرجل تسبيحا إذا عظم الله ومجده (jamhara)؛ السبحة التطوع من الذكر والصلاة (sihah)؛ السبحة من الصلاة التطوع (tahdhib)؛ التسبيح عاما في العبادات قولا كان أو فعلا أو نية (mufradat)
- **B002** Tanrı'yı her türlü eksiklikten uzak sayma — Tanrı her türlü kötülük ve eksiklikten uzaktır · Tanrı'yı her türlü kötülük ve eksiklikten uzak sayarak yüceltme · şaşma veya söz konusu kişiyi bir iddiadan uzak tutma sözü · her türlü kötülükten ve kendisine yakışmayan nitelikten uzak olan Tanrı
  التسبيح وهو تنزيه الله من كل سوء (maqayis)؛ سبحان الله تنزيه لله (ayn)؛ سبحان تنزيه وتبرئة (jamhara)؛ التسبيح التنزيه (sihah)؛ سبحان في اللغة تنزيه لله عز وجل عن السوء (tahdhib)؛ التسبيح تنزيه الله تعالى (mufradat)
- **B003** Tanrı'nın yüzünün görkemi, büyüklüğü ve ışığı — Tanrı'nın yüzünün görkemi, büyüklüğü ve ışığı · yere kapanma yerleri
  السبحات جلال الله وعظمته (maqayis)؛ سبحات وجه ربنا يعني جلاله وعظمته ونوره (ayn)؛ سبحات وجهه نور وجهه (jamhara)؛ سبحات وجه ربنا أي جلالته (sihah)؛ سبحات وجهه نور وجهه؛ السبحات مواضع السجود (tahdhib)
- **B004** yüzerek veya akıcı biçimde hızla ilerleme — yüzme ve su ya da havada hızla ilerleme · yıldızların yörüngede akıp ilerlemesi · ön ayaklarını ileri uzatarak koşan at · yörüngede ya da koşuda hızla akıp gidenler
  السَّبح والسباحة العوم في الماء (maqayis)؛ السبح مصدر كالسباحة سبح السابح في الماء (ayn)؛ سبح الرجل وغيره في الماء سبحا وسباحة (jamhara)؛ السباحة العوم (sihah)؛ النجوم تسبح في الفلك؛ السابح من الخيل يمد يديه في الجري (tahdhib)؛ السبح المر السريع في الماء وفي الهواء (mufradat)
- **B005** iş ve geçim için zaman ve hareket imkânı — serbest zaman, geçim için hareket ve gidip gelme imkânı · yeryüzünde uzaklara gitmek · sözü uzatıp çok konuşmak
  أصلان أحدهما جنس من العبادة والآخر جنس من السعي (maqayis)؛ سبحا طويلا أي فراغا للنوم (ayn)؛ السبح الفراغ والتصرف في المعاش والمنقلب والجيئة والذهاب (sihah)؛ فراغا وتصرفا؛ اضطرابا ومعاشا؛ منقلبا طويلا (tahdhib)؛ سرعة الذهاب في العمل (mufradat)
- **B006** anma sözlerini saymaya yarayan boncuk dizisi — Tanrı'yı anma sözlerini saymaya yarayan boncuk dizisi
  السبحة خرزات يسبح بعدها (ayn)؛ السبحة بالضم خرزات يسبح بها (sihah)؛ الخرزات التي يعد بها المسبح تسبيحه السبحة وهي كلمة مولدة (tahdhib)؛ الخرزات التي بها يسبح سبحة (mufradat)
- **B007** çocuk deri giysisi; güçlü ve sıkı örtü — çocuklar için deriden yapılmış gömlek veya giysi · güçlü, sağlam ve sıkı örtü
  السبحة قميص يعمل للصبيان من جلود وسلف رقيق والجمع سباح (jamhara)؛ السبحة بفتح السين وجمعها سباح ثياب من جلود؛ السباح قمص للصبيان من جلود؛ كساء مسبح أي قوي شديد (tahdhib)
- **B008** kutsal kent veya hac bölgesindeki bir vadinin adı — kutsal kent ya da hac sırasında durulan bölgedeki bir vadinin adı
  سَبّوحة البلد الحرام ويقال واد بعرفات (sihah)

## ط و ل (root_000959): 73:7 طَوِيلًا

- **B001** uzunluk ve boyca geçme — uzunluk · uzamak, uzun duruma gelmek · uzun, daha uzun ya da uzun boylu · birini boyca geçmek · üst dudağı alt dudağından uzun deve · bakmak için doğrulup başını ve gövdesini uzatmak · Kur'an'ın yedi uzun suresi · uzun boylu bir çocuk doğurmak
  أصل صحيح يدل على فضل وامتداد في الشيء (maqayis)؛ طال الشيء يطول طولا فهو طويل (maqayis;ayn;tahdhib)؛ الطول خلاف العرض (maqayis;sihah)؛ الأطول نقيض الأقصر (ayn;tahdhib)؛ الطول والقصر من الأسماء المتضايفة (mufradat)؛ طال فلان فلانا أي فاته في الطول (ayn;tahdhib)
- **B002** uzun otlatma ipi — uzun otlatma ipi · atın bağını merada gevşetmek · hayvanı otlatırken ayağına bağlanan uzun ip
  للحبل الطول لطوله وامتداده (maqayis)؛ الطول اسم حبل تشد به قوائم الدابة ثم ترسل في المرعى (ayn)؛ الحبل الذي يطول للدابة فترعى فيه (sihah;tahdhib)؛ الحبل المرخي على الدابة (mufradat)؛ طول فرسك أي أرخ له حبله في مرعاه (ayn;tahdhib)
- **B003** zamanın uzayıp gitmesi [kalıp] — bütün zaman boyunca · süresi uzamak, işte oyalanıp ağır davranmak
  لا أكلمه طوال الدهر (maqayis;ayn;sihah;tahdhib)؛ طال طولك إذا طال تماديه في أمر وتراخيه عنه (ayn;tahdhib)؛ طال طولك وطيلك أي طالت مدته (tahdhib)؛ يستعمل في الأعراض كالزمان (mufradat)؛ تطاول عليهم العمر (mufradat)
- **B004** güç, varlık ve iyilik üstünlüğü — güç, varlık, bolluk, üstünlük ve iyilik · güç ve bolluk sahibi · evlenme bedeli ve geçim giderini karşılayacak maddi güç · insanlara iyilik ve bağışla üstünlük göstermek
  يدل على فضل وامتداد (maqayis)؛ الطول القدرة وإن فلانا لذو طول أي ذو قدرة (ayn)؛ الطول بالفتح المن (sihah)؛ الطول هنا القدرة على المهر (tahdhib)؛ الطول الغنى والطول الفضل (tahdhib)؛ الطول خص به الفضل والمن (mufradat)؛ طولا كناية عما يصرف إلى المهر والنفقة (mufradat)
- **B005** işe yarar değer — işe yarar yanı ve kayda değer üstünlüğü olmayan iş · işe yarar değer ve ayırt edici üstünlük

- **B001** sonraki ya da öteki olan — sonraki; öteki · sonraki veya öteki olan dişil öğe · başkaları; ötekiler · insanların son kesimleri · zamanın sonu · ardından hiçbir şey gelmeyen son
  الآخر نقيض المتقدم؛ الآخر تال للأول؛ أخر جماعة أخرى (maqayis); هذا آخر وهذه أخرى؛ الآخر والآخرة نقيض المتقدم والمتقدمة؛ الآخر الغائب؛ أخر جماعة أخرى (ayn); الآخر بعد الأول؛ الآخر أحد الشيئين؛ الجمع أواخر؛ أخريات الناس أي أواخرهم؛ أخرى القوم أي من كان في آخرهم؛ أبعد الله الاخر (sihah); معنى آخر شيء غير الأول الذي قبله؛ أخر جماعة أخرى؛ أخرى القوم أي في أواخرهم (tahdhib); آخر يقابل به الأول، وآخر يقابل به الواحد؛ أخر معدول (mufradat)

  القدم ما يطأ عليه الإنسان (ayn;tahdhib)؛ القدم واحد الأقدام (sihah)؛ القدم قدم الرجل وجمعه أقدام (mufradat)؛ قدم الإنسان معروفة ولعلها سميت بذلك لأنها آلة للتقدم والسبق (maqayis)
- **B002** önceden oluşmuş etki veya paye — öncül etki, iyilik veya saygın konum · önceden kazanılmış iyi paye · önceden işlenmiş kötülük · önder ve saygın kişi · hükümdar veya önder
  القدمة والقدم أيضا السابقة في الأمر وللكافرين قدم شر (ayn)؛ القدم أيضا السابقة في الأمر ولفلان قدم صدق أي أثرة حسنة (sihah)؛ قدم الصدق المنزلة الرفيعة والقدم السابقة وكل ما قدمت من خير والعمل الصالح واليد والمعروف والصنيعة والشرف القديم (tahdhib)؛ متقدم على فلان أي أشرف منه (mufradat)؛ لفلان قدم صدق أي شيء متقدم من أثر حسن والملك هو المقدم (maqayis)؛ رجل قدموس سيد وهو ذلك المعنى (maqayis-variant)
- **B003** eskilik ve önceden var olma — eskilik; sonradan oluşmama · eski; geçmiş zamana dayanan · eskiden; geçmiş zamanda · eski olan
  القدم مصدر القديم من كل شيء (ayn)؛ قدم الشيء فهو قديم والقدم خلاف الحدوث وقدما كان كذا (sihah)؛ القدم العتق مصدر القديم وقد قدم يقدم (tahdhib)؛ حديث وقديم والقدم وجود فيما مضى (mufradat)؛ القدم خلاف الحدوث وشيء قديم إذا كان زمانه سالفا (maqayis)؛ القدموس القديم وأصله من القدم (maqayis-variant)
- **B004** öne geçme, önde ilerleme veya erken davranma — öne geçmek; önde olmak · öne geçme ve ilerleme · ön taraf; önde · önüne geçmek; vaktinden önce davranmak · duraksamadan ileri gitmek
  قدم فلان قومه أي يكون أمامهم والقدم المضي أمام ويمضي قدما أي لا ينثني وقدام خلاف وراء والقدم ضد الأخر (ayn)؛ قدم أي تقدم وقدم بين يديه أي تقدم ومضى قدما لم يعرج ولم ينثن واستقدم وتقدم بمعنى ومقدم نقيض مؤخر (sihah)؛ القدم المضي وهو الإقدام وقدام خلاف وراء والقدم ضد أخر وقدم فلان فلانا إذا تقدمه ولا تقدموا معناه لا تتقدموا قبل الوقت (tahdhib)؛ به اعتبر التقدم والتأخر (mufradat)؛ أصل صحيح يدل على سبق وأصله مضى فلان قدما لم يعرج ولم ينثن ومقدمة الجيش أوله (maqayis)
- **B005** yolculuktan dönüş — yolculuktan dönmek; geri gelmek · yolculuktan dönüş; yolcunun geliş zamanı · yolculuktan dönenler
  القدوم الرجوع من السفر وقدم يقدم (ayn)؛ قدم من سفره قدوما ومقدما والقدام القادمون من سفر (sihah)؛ القدوم الإياب من السفر وقدم فلان من سفره يقدم قدوما (tahdhib)؛ من الباب قدم من سفره قدوما ويقال القدام القادمون من سفر (maqayis)
- **B006** cesaretle öne atılma ve gözüpeklik — gözüpeklik; cesaretle öne atılma · bir işe cesaretle girişmek · gözüpek, atılgan savaşçı · ileri! · gözüpek ve öne atılan kişi
  رجل قدم مقتحم للأشياء يتقدم الناس ويمضي في الحرب قدما (ayn)؛ أقدم على الأمر إقداما والإقدام الشجاعة وأقدم زجر للفرس والمقدام الكثير الإقدام على العدو (sihah)؛ أقدم على قرنه إقداما وقدما ومقدما إذا تقدم عليه بجرأة وضده الإحجام ورجل مقدام في الحرب جريء (tahdhib)؛ أقدم على الشيء إقداما وأقدم زجر للفرس ومضى القوم في الحرب اليقدمية (maqayis)
- **B007** ön bölüm veya ilk kesim — ordunun öncü birliği · gözün veya başın ön kısmı · alın önü ve perçem bölgesi · kuşun ön kanat telekleri · eyer takımının ön kısmı · deve veya ineğin öndeki iki meme başı · dağın öne çıkan burnu · yüzüstü düşmek
  مقدم العين ما يلي الأنف والمقدمة الناصية وما استقبلك من الجبهة والجبين وقادمة الرحل والقادمة الريشة التي تلي منكب الجناح (ayn)؛ قوادم الطير مقاديم ريشه وقيدوم الجبل أنف يتقدم منه وقيدوم كل شيء مقدمه وصدره ومقدمة الجيش أوله وقادمتا الناقة (sihah)؛ مقدمة الجيش الذين يتقدمون الجيش ومقدم العين ومقدم الرأس والمقدمة الناصية وقادمة الرحل وللناقة قادمان وقوادم ريش الطائر (tahdhib)؛ قادمة الرحل خلاف آخرته وقادمة من أطباء الناقة وقادم الإنسان رأسه وقوادم الطير ومقدمة الجيش أوله وقيدوم الجبل أنف يتقدم منه (maqayis)
- **B008** ahşap yontma keseri — ahşap yontma keseri
  القدوم مخفقة الحديدة التي ينحت بها الخشب (ayn)؛ القدوم التي ينحت بها مخففة ولا تقل قدوم بالتشديد (sihah)؛ القدوم التي ينحت بها وجمعها قدم واختتن إبراهيم بالقدوم قال قطعه بها (tahdhib)؛ مما شذ عن هذا الأصل القدوم الحديدة ينحت بها وهي معروفة (maqayis)
- **B009** belirli bir yer adı — belirli bir yer adı
  القدوم أيضا اسم موضع (sihah)؛ القدوم مكان (maqayis)
- **B010** bir işe yönelip onu amaçlamak [kalıp] — bir işe yönelip onu amaçlamak
  قدم فلان إلى أمر كذا أي قصد له ومنه وقدمنآ إلى ما عملوا قال الفراء والزجاج قدمنا عمدنا وقصدنا (tahdhib)

## ن ف س (root_001533): 73:20 لِأَنفُسِكُم

- **B001** soluk alıp verme — soluk alıp verme · gövdeye girip çıkan hava; soluk · tek soluk ya da soluklanma arası · soluklar
  التنفس خروج النسيم من الجوف (maqayis;ayn); النفس واحد الأنفاس وكل ذي رئة متنفس (sihah); التنفس في الإناء وثلاثة أنفاس (tahdhib); النفس الريح الداخل والخارج في البدن من الفم والمنخر (mufradat)
- **B002** sıkıntıyı hafifletip ferahlatma — Tanrı onun sıkıntısını giderdi · beni sıkıntıdan kurtarıp rahatlat · Tanrı'nın sıkıntıdakilere ferahlık getiren esintisi ya da yardımı
  نفس الله كربته والنفس كل شيء يفرج به عن مكروب (maqayis); نفست عنه تنفيسا أي رفهت (sihah); اللهم نفس عني أي فرج عني والريح من نفس الرحمن (tahdhib;mufradat)
- **B003** kem gözle zarar verme — zarar verdiğine inanılan bakış · ona kem göz değdi · kem gözle zarar veren kişi
  يقال للعين نفس وأصابت فلانا نفس (maqayis); النفس العين ونفسته بنفس إذا أصبته بعين والنافس العائن (sihah); النفس العين التي تصيب المعين وإن فلانا لنفوس أي عيون (tahdhib)
- **B004** canlıdaki akışkan kan — kaybıyla yaşamın da yitirildiği kan · hayvandaki akışkan kan
  النفس الدم وإذا فقد الدم فقد نفسه (maqayis); النفس الدم وما ليس له نفس سائلة (sihah); النفس الدم وكل شيء له نفس سائلة أراد دما سائلا (tahdhib)
- **B005** doğum ve doğuma bağlı kadın-çocuk durumu — doğum yapmış ya da doğum sonrası kanaması olan kadın · doğum ve doğum sonrası kanama dönemi · yeni doğan çocuk · doğmadan önce
  الحائض تسمى النفساء والنفاس ولاد المرأة والولد منفوس (maqayis); النفاس ولادة المرأة فإذا وضعت كانت نفساء (ayn;mufradat); نفست المرأة غلاما والولد منفوس وورث قبل أن ينفس أي يولد (sihah); نفست المرأة إذا حاضت وأنفست أراد أحضت (tahdhib)
- **B006** soluk aralı içim ve bir içimlik yudum — bir solukta alınan yudum ya da içim · üç soluk arasıyla içme
  كرع في الإناء نفسا أو نفسين (maqayis); شربت الماء بنفس وثلاثة أنفاس وكل مستراح منه نفس (ayn); النفس الجرعة اكرع في الإناء نفسا أو نفسين (sihah); يشرب الماء وغيره بثلاث أنفاس (tahdhib)
- **B007** bir deri işlemeye yetecek sepi maddesi payı — deriyi bir kez işlemeye yetecek sepi maddesi · bir işlemelik sepi maddesi payı
  في الدباغ نفس قدر ما يدبغ به الإهاب مرة (maqayis); النفس قدر دبغة مما يدبغ به الأديم (sihah); النفس قدر دبغة أو دبغتين من الدباغ (tahdhib)
- **B008** yaşamı sürdüren, bol ve doyurucu su — yaşamı ayakta tutan su · bol ve susuzluğu gideren içecek · tadı kötü, bayat ve içimi güç içecek
  يقال للماء نفس ولأن قوام النفس به (maqayis); النفس الماء وشراب ذو نفس أي فيه سعة وري وشراب غير ذي نفس (tahdhib)
- **B009** kalıba bağlı yarılıp açılma ve genişleme [kalıp] — yay çatladı ya da yarıldı · sabah söktü, aydınlık yayıldı · gündüz uzayıp genişledi · ırmağın suyu artıp yayıldı
  تنفست القوس انشقت (maqayis); تنفس الصبح أي تبلج وتنفس النهار إذا زاد والموج إذا نضح الماء (sihah); إذا انشق الفجر وانفلق وتنفس دجلة إذا زاد ماؤها (tahdhib); تنفس النهار عبارة عن توسعه (mufradat)
- **B010** değerli ve uğrunda yarışılan şey — değerli, önemli ve arzulanan · bir şeyi elde etme ya da üstünlere benzeme yarışı · başkasına vermeye kıyamayıp esirgemek · sahip olduğu şey yüzünden onu kıskanmak
  شيء نفيس ذو نفس وخطر يتنافس به والتنافس يبرز كل واحد قوة نفسه (maqayis); شيء نفيس متنافس فيه ونفست به ضننت (ayn); نافست في الشيء إذا رغبت فيه وتنافسوا فيه ونفس به أي ضن أو حسد (sihah); مال نفيس ومنفس وكل شيء له خطر وقدر ونفس عليك أي حسدك (tahdhib); المنافسة مجاهدة النفس للتشبه بالأفاضل ونفست بكذا ضنت نفسي به وشيء نفيس (mufradat)
- **B011** bedene yaşam veren can — bedene yaşam veren can · insan ya da canlı birey
  النفس الروح الذي به حياة الجسد وكل إنسان نفس (ayn); النفس الروح يقال خرجت نفسه (sihah); خرجت نفس فلان أي روحه ونفس الحياة هي الروح (tahdhib); النفس الروح في قوله أخرجوا أنفسكم (mufradat)
- **B012** şeyin kendisi ve bütün öz varlığı [kalıp] — şeyin tam kendisi ve gerçeği · başkası aracılığıyla değil, bizzat kendisi
  كل شيء بعينه نفس (ayn); نفس الشيء عينه يؤكد به (sihah); معنى النفس حقيقة الشيء وجملته وذاته كلها وعين الشيء وكنهه وجوهره (tahdhib); نفسه ذاته (mufradat)
- **B013** iç düşünce, niyet ve ayırt etme gücü — onun içinden geçen düşünce ya da niyet · ayırt etmeyi sağlayan zihinsel güç
  نفس العقل التي يكون بها التمييز وفي نفس فلان أن يفعل أي في روعه وتعلم ما في نفسي أي ما عندي أو غيبك (tahdhib); يعلم ما في أنفسكم وتعلم ما في نفسي ولا أعلم ما في نفسك (mufradat)
- **B014** sağlam, cömert ve onurlu yaradılış — sağlam karakterli, dayanıklı ve cömert adam · büyüklük duygusu, onur, yüksek amaç ve kendine saygı
  رجل له نفس أي خلق وجلادة وسخاء (ayn); النفس العظمة والكبر والعزة والهمة والأنفة (tahdhib)
- **B015** uzaklık, genişlik ve zaman payı — işinde rahat hareket edecek genişlikte · ek süre ya da hareket alanı · daha uzak, daha uzun ya da daha geniş
  هذا المكان أنفس من ذاك أي أبعد شيئا (ayn); أنت في نفس من أمرك أي في سعة ولك في هذا الأمر نفسة أي مهلة (sihah); هذا المنزل أنفس أي أبعد وكتبت كتابا نفسا أي طويلا وزد في أجلي نفسا وبين الفريقين نفس أي متسع (tahdhib)
- **B016** eski bahis oyunundaki beşinci pay oku — eski bahis oyunundaki beşinci pay oku; bir aktarıma göre dördüncü ok
  النافس الخامس من القداح (ayn); النافس الخامس من سهام الميسر ويقال هو الرابع (sihah); النافس الخامس من قداح الميسر وفيه خمسة فروض (tahdhib)

## خ ي ر (root_000452): 73:20 خَيْرٍ, 73:20 خَيْرًا

- **B001** arzulanan iyilik — iyilik; yarar veya üstünlük taşıyan olumlu şey
  فالخير خلاف الشر لأن كل أحد يميل إليه (maqayis)؛ الخير ضد الشر (jamhara;sihah)؛ الخير ما يرغب فيه الكل وضده الشر (mufradat)؛ يقابل به الشر مرة والضر مرة (mufradat)
- **B002** iyi ve seçkin olma — iyi ve üstün nitelikli · üstün, güzel veya seçkin olan · üstün veya seçkin kimse ya da şey · iyi ve erdemli kişiler · üstün, güzel veya seçilmiş olanlar
  رجل خير وامرأة خيرة فاضلة وقوم خيار وأخيار في صلاحها وامرأة خيرة في جمالها وميسمها (maqayis;ayn)؛ رجل خير إذا كان فيه خير ورجل خيار من قوم خيار وأخيار والأخيار خلاف الأشرار (jamhara)؛ الخيرات جمع خيرة وهي الفاضلة من كل شيء (sihah)؛ فيهن مختارات لا رذل فيهن والخير الفاضل المختص بالخير (mufradat)
- **B003** daha iyi olanı seçme — seçim veya seçim hakkı · seçim, seçilmiş şey veya seçim sonucu · daha iyi olanı arayıp seçme · seçmek veya üstün tutmak · Yaratıcıdan kişi için iyi sonucu dilemek · Yaratıcının kişi için iyi olanı seçip vermesi · iki şey arasında seçim hakkını ona bırakmak · seçimde üstün gelmek veya diğerini geçmek · seçen ya da seçilmiş olan
  الخيرة الخيار والاستخارة أن تسأل خير الأمرين لك ويقال خايرت فلانا فخرته وتقول اختر (maqayis)؛ خايرت فلانا فخرته والله يخير للعبد إذا استخاره وهذا وهذه وهؤلاء خيرتي وهو ما تختاره (ayn)؛ الخيار الاسم من الاختيار والخيرة من قولك خار الله لك والاختيار الاصطفياء والاستخارة الخيرة وخيرته بين الشيئين (sihah)؛ الاختيار طلب ما هو خير وفعله واستخار الله العبد فخار له وخايرت فلانا كذا فخرته (mufradat)
- **B004** mal, özellikle çok veya övülen bir yoldan edinilmiş servet — mal, özellikle çok veya iyi yoldan edinilmiş servet
  إن ترك خيرا أي مالا (sihah;mufradat)؛ لا يقال للمال خير حتى يكون كثيرا ومن مكان طيب (mufradat)؛ وإنه لحب الخير لشديد أي المال الكثير (mufradat)؛ ما كان مجموعا من المال من وجه محمود (mufradat)
- **B005** cömertlik ve armağan verme — cömertlik, armağan ve verme
  والخير الكرم (maqayis)؛ الخير الهبة (ayn)؛ رجل ذو خير إذا كان كثير الخير (jamhara)؛ الخير بالكسر الكرم (sihah)
- **B006** bir geçidi tıkayıp hayvanı yuvasından çıkarma [kalıp] — sırtlanı, yuvasının bir geçidini tıkayarak başka çıkıştan çıkarma · çöl sıçanını, yuvasının bir geçidini tıkayarak başka çıkıştan çıkarma
  استخاره الضبع وهو أن تجعل خشبة في ثقبة بيتها حتى تخرج من مكان إلى آخر (maqayis)؛ يستخير الضبع واليربوع إذا جعل في موضع النافقاء فخرج من القاصعاء (ayn)

## و ج د (root_001626): 73:20 تَجِدُوهُ

- **B001** bulma ve duyusal ya da zihinsel olarak algılama — bir şeyi bulmak veya ona erişmek · yitiği bulmak · bulma ve erişme · birini aradığı şeye ulaştırmak · duyularla veya akılla algılama · onları gördüğünüz veya kendilerine eriştiğiniz yerde
  الشيء يلفيه (maqayis)؛ وجدت الضالة وجدانا (maqayis;sihah)؛ الوجدان والجدة من قولك وجدت الشيء أي أصبته (ayn)؛ وجدت الشيء أجده وجدانا (jamhara)؛ وجد مطلوبه يجده وجودا (sihah)؛ الوجود أضرب: وجود بإحدى الحواس الخمس ... ووجود بالعقل (mufradat)؛ حيث وجدتموهم أي حيث رأيتموهم (mufradat)

  وجد الشيء عن عدم فهو موجود (sihah)؛ أوجده الله (sihah)؛ الموجودات ثلاثة أضرب (mufradat)
- **B003** varlıklı veya yeterli olmak; varlıklı ya da güçlü kılmak — maddi genişlik ve varlıklılık · malca genişleyip varlıklı olmak · varlıklı ve ödeme gücü bulunan kimse · onu varlıklı kılmak · onu zayıflıktan sonra güçlendirmek · imkanınız ve maddi gücünüz ölçüsünde · suya ulaşmaya gücünüz yetmediyse
  الوجدان والجدة من قولك وجدت الشيء أي أصبته (ayn)؛ وجدت في المال جدة ووجدا ووجدا؛ الواجد الغني (jamhara)؛ وجد في المال وجدا ووجدا وجدة أي استغنى؛ أوجده أي أغناه؛ آجدني بعد ضعف أي قواني (sihah)؛ يعبر عن التمكن من الشيء بالوجود؛ من وجدكم أي تمكنكم وقدر غناكم (mufradat)

  جبر العظم الكسير (maqayis)؛ الأجور جبر الكسر على عوج العظم (ayn)؛ أجر العظم ... برأ على عثم (sihah)؛ أجر الكسر ... إذا برأ على اعوجاج (tahdhib)؛ الإجارة ... القافية طاء والأخرى دالا ... من أجور الكسر (tahdhib)

(source: latent_activation/network/v3/reviews/s073/reader_a_pilot.md)

# s073 Semantic Channel Discovery

## Parent Channels

### 1. P1 - Measured Night Formation
- Semantic invariant: A demanding practice is formed by partitioning duration, setting the body upright, and ordering speech into an exact sequence.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:2-6 anchors `قُمِ`, `ٱلَّيْلَ`, `قَلِيلًا`, `نِّصْفَهُ`, `ٱنقُصْ`, `زِدْ`, `رَتِّلِ`, `قُرْءَانَ`, `قَوْلًا`, `وَطْـًٔا`, and `أَقْوَمُ`.
- Surprising reach: The time instructions behave not only as scheduling but as compositional work: subtracting, adding, balancing, gathering, and aligning a verbal whole.

#### Subchannel A. Partitioning the night
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A night interval is divided, reduced, or enlarged while the practitioner rises within it.
- Active motifs: night and its darkness (`quranic:root_001392:B001/m01`); nighttime activity (`quranic:root_001392:B002/m01`); bodily standing (`quranic:root_001273:B002/m01`); a small portion (`quranic:root_001251:B001/m01`); one half (`quranic:root_001511:B001/m01`); reaching the midpoint (`quranic:root_001511:B002/m01`); removal of part (`quranic:root_001542:B001/m01`); increase and growth (`quranic:root_000657:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:2 `قُمِ/ٱلَّيْلَ/قَلِيلًا`; 73:3 `نِّصْفَهُ/ٱنقُصْ/قَلِيلًا`; 73:4 `زِدْ`; 73:6 `ٱلَّيْلِ/أَقْوَمُ`
- Synthesis: The interval is treated as workable material. Standing supplies the operative posture, while fewness, half, decrease, and increase make the night a deliberately adjustable portion rather than an undifferentiated span.

#### Subchannel B. Calibrating weight and correspondence
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Quantities and bodily-verbal contact are brought into balance, agreement, and stable proportion.
- Active motifs: known weight and measure (`quranic:root_000202:B004/m01`); equal weight and moderate quantity (`quranic:root_001273:B015/m01`); fairness and equal dealing (`quranic:root_001511:B003/m01`); thing-to-thing correspondence (`quranic:root_001659:B004/m01`); ordered alignment (`quranic:root_000540:B002/m01`); straightness and equilibrium (`quranic:root_001273:B008/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:3 `نِّصْفَهُ`; 73:4 `رَتِّلِ/تَرْتِيلًا`; 73:5 `ثَقِيلًا`; 73:6 `وَطْـًٔا/أَقْوَمُ`
- Synthesis: The lexical field converts the regimen into calibration. Weight is matched to measure, half evokes equitable division, and the night’s stronger contact becomes correspondence among elements rather than pressure alone.

#### Subchannel C. Assembling and transmitting ordered speech
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Discrete verbal material is gathered, articulated in sequence, delivered, and received as instruction.
- Active motifs: deliberate articulated recitation (`quranic:root_000540:B001/m01`); gathering parts into a whole (`quranic:root_001210:B001/m01`); reading and recitation (`quranic:root_001210:B002/m01`); collected wording and reading (`quranic:root_001211:B001/m01`); spoken utterance (`quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`); receiving and learning words (`quranic:root_001372:B011/m01`); ear-heart-tongue agreement (`quranic:root_001659:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:4 `رَتِّلِ/ٱلْقُرْءَانَ/تَرْتِيلًا`; 73:5 `نُلْقِى/قَوْلًا`; 73:6 `وَطْـًٔا/قِيلًا`
- Synthesis: Recitation is an assembly line of signification: gathering precedes ordering, ordered wording is cast toward a recipient, and reception completes the process. The correspondence sense of `و ط ء` makes the result an alignment of hearing, inward grasp, and tongue.

### 2. P1 - Burden Transmission and Bodily Traction
- Semantic invariant: A load becomes effective when a carrier rises, braces, receives it, and converts pressure into stable contact.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:1-6 anchors `ٱلْمُزَّمِّلُ`, `قُمِ`, `نُلْقِى`, `قَوْلًا ثَقِيلًا`, `أَشَدُّ`, and `وَطْـًٔا`.
- Surprising reach: The “heavy saying” is materialized as freight, pregnancy, impaired hearing, a thrown object, and the force of feet finding purchase.

#### Subchannel A. The wrapped load-bearer
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A wrapped figure rises like a carrier who must brace under freight.
- Active motifs: wrapping oneself in clothes (`quranic:root_000644:B003/m01`); load and weight (`quranic:root_000644:B001/m01`); pillion and balanced riding (`quranic:root_000644:B002/m01`); an animal bearing forward on its forelegs (`quranic:root_000644:B005/m01`); lifting and carrying a load (`quranic:root_001251:B004/m01`); bodily standing (`quranic:root_001273:B002/m01`); undertaking an affair (`quranic:root_001273:B003/m01`); physical and semantic heaviness (`quranic:root_000202:B001/m01`); sluggish burdened movement (`quranic:root_000202:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:1 `ٱلْمُزَّمِّلُ`; 73:2 `قُمِ`; 73:5 `ثَقِيلًا`
- Synthesis: Clothing, freight, riding balance, and braced gait converge on an embodied carrier. The address to the wrapped one is thereby pressured by a latent transport image: rising means assuming and stabilizing a load.

#### Subchannel B. Casting and receiving the weighty saying
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A valuable, weight-bearing utterance is cast toward a hearer and taken up as learned speech.
- Active motifs: throwing something onto another (`quranic:root_001372:B005/m01`); spoken utterance (`quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`); a precious thing or saying of weight (`quranic:root_000202:B005/m01`); receiving and learning words (`quranic:root_001372:B011/m01`); taking hold of spoken words (`quranic:root_000018:B001/m01`); heaviness in the ear (`quranic:root_000202:B009/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:5 `نُلْقِى/قَوْلًا/ثَقِيلًا`; 73:6 `قِيلًا`; 73:9 `فَٱتَّخِذْهُ`
- Synthesis: Delivery is simultaneously projectile transfer and instruction. The saying has precious weight, but that weight can also tax hearing, so successful reception requires active taking rather than passive exposure.

#### Subchannel C. Foot pressure becoming firm contact
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A footfall presses down, meets resistance, and settles into a firm, congruent stance.
- Active motifs: foot treading a place (`quranic:root_001659:B001/m01`); severe pressure or seizure (`quranic:root_001659:B003/m01`); thing-to-thing agreement (`quranic:root_001659:B004/m01`); fastening and strengthening a bond (`quranic:root_000782:B001/m01`); force, solidity, and exertion (`quranic:root_000782:B002/m01`); upright stability (`quranic:root_001273:B008/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:6 `أَشَدُّ/وَطْـًٔا/أَقْوَمُ`
- Synthesis: The night’s “tread” unfolds as a mechanics of formation. Pressure alone would crush, but binding, solidity, and correspondence convert it into footing that can support straighter speech.

### 3. P1 - Withdrawal and Reassigned Guardianship
- Semantic invariant: Cutting one relation does not leave a vacuum; the released affair is redirected toward an endpoint and entrusted to a keeper.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:8-10 anchors `تَبَتَّلْ`, `إِلَيْهِ`, `فَٱتَّخِذْهُ وَكِيلًا`, `ٱصْبِرْ`, and `ٱهْجُرْهُمْ هَجْرًا جَمِيلًا`.
- Surprising reach: Separation extends from severance and migration to a self-sufficient palm offshoot, while reliance is tested against failed mutual delegation and lagging dependence.

#### Subchannel A. Severance directed toward an endpoint
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A participant cuts loose from competing attachments and directs the separation toward a terminal relation.
- Active motifs: cutting and distinguishing one thing from another (`quranic:root_000082:B001/m01`); abstention from marital attachment (`quranic:root_000082:B003/m01`); a palm offshoot separated from its mother (`quranic:root_000082:B004/m01`); separation and bodily, verbal, or inward departure (`quranic:root_001578:B001/m01`); exile and removal from one’s place (`quranic:root_001077:B007/m01`); reaching an endpoint (`quranic:root_000076:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:8 `تَبَتَّلْ/تَبْتِيلًا`; 73:9 `ٱلْمَغْرِبِ`; 73:10 `ٱهْجُرْهُمْ/هَجْرًا`; surface anchor unavailable for `quranic:root_000076:B001` because `ء ل ي` is missing from root coverage
- Synthesis: The scene is not mere isolation. Like an offshoot that has become self-standing, separation removes dependency so movement can terminate in a newly exclusive attachment.

#### Subchannel B. Beautiful non-engagement
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Hostile speech is met by restrained departure conducted with fairness and visible grace.
- Active motifs: holding the self back from panic or reaction (`quranic:root_000840:B001/m01`); leaving and distancing without continued exchange (`quranic:root_001578:B001/m01`); beauty in conduct and action (`quranic:root_000260:B004/m01`); fairness and equal dealing (`quranic:root_001511:B003/m01`); good reputation produced by conduct (`quranic:root_000516:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:3 `نِّصْفَهُ`; 73:8 `ٱذْكُرِ`; 73:10 `ٱصْبِرْ/ٱهْجُرْهُمْ/هَجْرًا/جَمِيلًا`
- Synthesis: Departure is shaped as an ethical performance rather than a rupture driven by rage. Restraint, equitable dealing, and beauty specify the manner in which the relation is ended and the kind of public memory it leaves.

#### Subchannel C. Entrusting the released affair
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: What is no longer personally grasped is delegated to a sovereign caretaker who can preserve and discharge it.
- Active motifs: taking something as one’s own (`quranic:root_000018:B010/m01`); handing an affair to another (`quranic:root_001681:B001/m01`); reliance (`quranic:root_001681:B002/m01`); a sufficient guardian and guarantor (`quranic:root_001681:B006/m01`); ownership and sovereignty (`quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`); repair, nurture, and completion (`quranic:root_000532:B002/m01`); surety and accompanying responsibility (`quranic:root_000840:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:8-9 `رَبِّكَ/رَّبُّ/فَٱتَّخِذْهُ/وَكِيلًا`; 73:10 `ٱصْبِرْ`
- Synthesis: “Taking a guardian” reverses ordinary possession: the human act of taking installs another as the true holder of the affair. Sovereignty is joined to nurture and surety, distinguishing reliable guardianship from dependence that merely abandons responsibility.

#### Subchannel D. Failed delegation and exhausted dependence
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Delegation fails when a weak dependent cannot bear its load, counterparties neglect one another, or a carrier lags and stops.
- Active motifs: a weak dependent (`quranic:root_001681:B003/m01`); mutual neglect after reciprocal delegation (`quranic:root_001681:B004/m01`); a lagging animal dependent on the herd (`quranic:root_001681:B005/m01`); a weak or deficient burden (`quranic:root_000644:B004/m01`); an exhausted animal stopping after travel (`quranic:root_001273:B016/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:1 `ٱلْمُزَّمِّلُ`; 73:2,6 `قُمِ/أَقْوَمُ`; 73:9 `وَكِيلًا`
- Synthesis: These are failure cases for guardianship. A dependent needs support, but reciprocal abandonment leaves the load unattended; the lagging and exhausted animals show the bodily consequence when delegated support does not hold.

### 4. P1 - Speech From Inward Form to Public Circulation
- Semantic invariant: A sign passes through inward conception, articulation, social circulation, and durable naming.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:4-10 anchors `رَتِّلِ`, `قَوْلًا`, `قِيلًا`, `ٱذْكُرِ`, `ٱسْمَ`, and `يَقُولُونَ`.
- Surprising reach: Speech can become rumor, false attribution, delirium, doctrine, definition, reputation, or a document of right, so verbal pressure is also pressure over social reality.

#### Subchannel A. From inner saying to articulated sign
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An inwardly held representation is externalized by the tongue and made to indicate or define an object.
- Active motifs: an unspoken saying in the self (`quranic:root_001272:B012/m01`); spoken utterance (`quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`); the tongue as speech instrument (`quranic:root_001272:B002/m01`); a thing’s signifying “saying” (`quranic:root_001272:B014/m01`); a defining formulation (`quranic:root_001272:B016/m01`); inward recollection (`quranic:root_000516:B003/m01`); verbal mention (`quranic:root_000516:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:5-6 `قَوْلًا/قِيلًا`; 73:8 `ٱذْكُرِ`; 73:10 `يَقُولُونَ`
- Synthesis: Saying begins before sound and ends beyond it. Inward representation becomes tongue-borne wording, then functions as indication and definition, giving the commands to remember and recite a full cognitive-discursive depth.

#### Subchannel B. Rumor, fabrication, and verbal injury
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Speech detaches from verified reality, circulates among people, and wounds through false attribution or obscenity.
- Active motifs: habitual profuse talk (`quranic:root_001272:B003/m01`); saying what did not occur or attributing it falsely (`quranic:root_001272:B005/m01`); circulating public talk (`quranic:root_001272:B007/m01`); negotiation and verbal exchange (`quranic:root_001272:B009/m01`); obscene or injurious speech (`quranic:root_001578:B003/m01`); unintended delirious speech (`quranic:root_001578:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:5-6 `قَوْلًا/قِيلًا`; 73:10 `يَقُولُونَ/ٱهْجُرْهُمْ`
- Synthesis: The opponents’ saying opens onto a whole circulation ecology: repetition, bargaining, false imputation, and abusive speech. Delirium marks the limiting case in which words continue while accountable intention has collapsed.

#### Subchannel C. Name, reminder, and reputation
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Naming calls an absent referent into social presence and repeated mention stabilizes it as memory and reputation.
- Active motifs: name as designation (`quranic:root_000745:B005/m01`); memory restored and retained (`quranic:root_000516:B003/m01`); verbal mention (`quranic:root_000516:B004/m01`); reminder that restores presence (`quranic:root_000516:B009/m01`); public honor and reputation (`quranic:root_000516:B007/m01`); a good report spreading (`quranic:root_000745:B008/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:8 `ٱذْكُرِ/ٱسْمَ`; 73:9 `رَبُّ`
- Synthesis: Remembering the name is a repeatable act that carries presence through time. The same machinery can create public repute, so private remembrance and social renown are related outcomes of sustained mention.

#### Subchannel D. Appropriating a saying as conviction
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Received speech is taken inward, weighed as a supposition, and either adopted as doctrine or held with sincere concern.
- Active motifs: taking a saying inward as one’s own (`quranic:root_001272:B006/m01`); supposition and opinion (`quranic:root_001272:B011/m01`); belief or doctrine (`quranic:root_001272:B013/m01`); sincere concern expressed in speech (`quranic:root_001272:B015/m01`); receiving and learning words (`quranic:root_001372:B011/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:5-6 `نُلْقِى/قَوْلًا/قِيلًا`; 73:10 `يَقُولُونَ`
- Synthesis: This scene separates hearing from assent. A saying can be received, entertained as an opinion, and appropriated as a settled conviction; sincere concern marks a morally different mode of inward uptake from rumor or fabrication.

### 5. P1 - Horizon Cycle and Traversal
- Semantic invariant: Time is perceived as movement across a bounded expanse, whether by sun, traveler, swimmer, mount, or caravan.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:2, 7, and 9 anchor `ٱلَّيْلَ`, `ٱلنَّهَارِ`, `سَبْحًا طَوِيلًا`, `ٱلْمَشْرِقِ`, and `ٱلْمَغْرِبِ`.
- Surprising reach: The long daytime “swim” becomes both livelihood-roaming and a transport scene in which the night itself is ridden as a camel.

#### Subchannel A. The horizon as a clock
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Light rises, reaches its height, declines, and gives way to night, making direction a temporal measure.
- Active motifs: sunrise and the eastern direction (`quranic:root_000790:B001/m01`); sunset and the western direction (`quranic:root_001077:B006/m01`); night and darkness (`quranic:root_001392:B001/m01`); daylight opening in brightness (`quranic:root_001559:B002/m01`); the sun standing at noon (`quranic:root_001273:B017/m01`); the day at its height (`quranic:root_000782:B005/m01`); extended duration (`quranic:root_000959:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:2,6 `ٱلَّيْلَ/ٱلَّيْلِ`; 73:6 `أَشَدُّ/أَقْوَمُ`; 73:7 `ٱلنَّهَارِ/طَوِيلًا`; 73:9 `ٱلْمَشْرِقِ/ٱلْمَغْرِبِ`
- Synthesis: East and west are temporal gates, while noon supplies a vertical midpoint. The cycle links nightly standing to a daylight arc whose length is read through the moving light.

#### Subchannel B. Riding through night and roaming by day
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A traveler mounts the night, moves with companions, and uses the day’s breadth for livelihood.
- Active motifs: nighttime travel (`quranic:root_001392:B002/m01`); taking the night as a camel for continuous travel (`quranic:root_000260:B008/m01`); a camel herd (`quranic:root_000260:B001/m01`); swimming, running, or extended motion (`quranic:root_000666:B004/m01`); free movement for livelihood (`quranic:root_000666:B005/m01`); a traveling company (`quranic:root_000644:B009/m01`); travel provisions (`quranic:root_000657:B005/m01`); distance and estrangement (`quranic:root_001077:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:1 `ٱلْمُزَّمِّلُ`; 73:2,6 `ٱلَّيْلَ/ٱلَّيْلِ`; 73:4 `زِدْ`; 73:7 `سَبْحًا`; 73:9 `ٱلْمَغْرِبِ`; 73:10 `جَمِيلًا`
- Synthesis: The day-night contrast becomes a travel economy. Night is a mount to be ridden with endurance; day is open roaming for livelihood, supplied by provisions and exposed to distance from home.

#### Subchannel C. Sky movement and layered weather
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: High, layered clouds gather, persist, and move above the day-night route.
- Active motifs: sky, cloud, and what rises overhead (`quranic:root_000745:B004/m01`); layered rain cloud (`quranic:root_000532:B008/m01`); stacked white cloud (`quranic:root_000840:B010/m01`); cloud named `النَّاهُور` (`quranic:root_001559:B008/m01`); rising and elevation (`quranic:root_001502:B001/m01`); a high object visible at distance (`quranic:root_000745:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:6 `نَاشِئَةَ`; 73:7 `ٱلنَّهَارِ`; 73:8 `ٱسْمَ/رَبِّكَ`; 73:10 `ٱصْبِرْ`
- Synthesis: Elevation links the night’s rising to meteorological formation. Persisting cloud layers supply a visible model of accumulation above the horizontal journey, turning temporal endurance into a sky-borne structure.

### 6. P1 - Water Capture and Conveyance
- Semantic invariant: Water becomes usable through a linked system of source, channel, lifting vessel, receiving basin, and controlled flow.
- Surface relation: indirect; 73:5-9 anchors `نُلْقِى`, `نَاشِئَةَ`, `سَبْحًا`, `رَبِّكَ`, and `ٱلْمَغْرِبِ`.
- Surprising reach: The vocabulary assembles an almost complete hydraulic installation beneath passages usually read through worship, speech, and cosmic direction.

#### Subchannel A. Well, bucket, trough, and reservoir
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Water is drawn from a well, caught in a vessel, and retained in a trough or basin.
- Active motifs: a large well bucket (`quranic:root_001077:B002/m01`); water spilling from well buckets (`quranic:root_001077:B003/m01`); a basin that retains water (`quranic:root_000018:B006/m01`); the first-built components of a trough (`quranic:root_001502:B007/m01`); a large jar (`quranic:root_001251:B003/m01`); abundant gathered water (`quranic:root_000532:B013/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:2-3 `قَلِيلًا`; 73:5 `نُلْقِى`; 73:6 `نَاشِئَةَ`; 73:8-9 `رَبِّكَ/رَبُّ/ٱلْمَغْرِبِ`
- Synthesis: The scene supplies distinct components rather than a loose water theme. Bucket, overflow, jar, trough masonry, basin, and stored abundance form a coherent capture-and-retention mechanism.

#### Subchannel B. Opening and directing a channel
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A passage is opened and widened so retained water can be directed into a running channel.
- Active motifs: a river cutting the earth (`quranic:root_001559:B001/m01`); opening and widening until liquid flows (`quranic:root_001559:B003/m01`); swimming or flowing motion (`quranic:root_000666:B004/m01`); joining two ends (`quranic:root_001372:B010/m01`); a tree-lined watercourse in a valley (`quranic:root_001511:B007/m01`); gathered water (`quranic:root_000532:B013/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:3 `نِّصْفَهُ`; 73:5 `نُلْقِى`; 73:7 `سَبْحًا/ٱلنَّهَارِ`; 73:8-9 `رَبِّكَ/رَبُّ`
- Synthesis: The channel is a continuous mechanism: openings are widened, endpoints joined, and water carried through a valley course. The daytime “swim” is thereby materialized as directed flow rather than motion without a bed.

#### Subchannel C. A loaded vessel under a captain
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Cargo and rigging are carried over water while a captain directs the vessel beneath waves and sky.
- Active motifs: thick ship rope (`quranic:root_000260:B002/m01`); a captain of sailors (`quranic:root_000532:B017/m01`); wave crest or high back (`quranic:root_001077:B009/m01`); swimming through water (`quranic:root_000666:B004/m01`); freight and load (`quranic:root_000644:B001/m01`); upright mechanical part (`quranic:root_001273:B012/m01`); sky overhead (`quranic:root_000745:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:1 `ٱلْمُزَّمِّلُ`; 73:2,6 `قُمِ/أَقْوَمُ`; 73:7 `سَبْحًا`; 73:8-9 `ٱسْمَ/رَبِّكَ/رَبُّ/ٱلْمَغْرِبِ`; 73:10 `جَمِيلًا`
- Synthesis: Rope, freight, upright gear, wave, and captain complete a maritime role map. The lexical captain extends divine guardianship into skilled navigation of a loaded vessel.

### 7. P1 - Proportion, Bodily Beauty, and Ethical Grace
- Semantic invariant: Beauty arises when distinct parts are properly spaced, balanced, and coordinated into a gracious whole.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:3-4 and 73:10 anchor `نِّصْفَهُ`, `رَتِّلِ`, and `هَجْرًا جَمِيلًا`.
- Surprising reach: “Beautiful abandonment” is reinforced by a concrete aesthetics of even teeth, balanced facial features, distinct limbs, color, and stature.

#### Subchannel A. A well-proportioned body and face
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Teeth, facial features, limbs, color, hair, and stature are arranged into a balanced visible form.
- Active motifs: even, well-spaced teeth (`quranic:root_000540:B003/m01`); balanced facial features (`quranic:root_001511:B009/m01`); distinct and complete limbs (`quranic:root_000082:B005/m01`); bodily stature (`quranic:root_001273:B011/m01`); strongly saturated color (`quranic:root_000790:B003/m01`); white eyelashes or hair (`quranic:root_001077:B012/m01`); tooth edges and moisture (`quranic:root_001077:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:3 `نِّصْفَهُ`; 73:4 `رَتِّلِ/تَرْتِيلًا`; 73:6 `أَقْوَمُ`; 73:8 `تَبَتَّلْ`; 73:9 `ٱلْمَشْرِقِ/ٱلْمَغْرِبِ`
- Synthesis: The scene is anatomically specific: spacing governs teeth, symmetry governs the face, separation defines limbs, and color and hair complete appearance. Ordered recitation thus resonates with an ordered visible body.

#### Subchannel B. Grace as proportionate conduct
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Ethical action is held to a balanced measure, neither retaliatory excess nor degrading retreat.
- Active motifs: beauty in action (`quranic:root_000260:B004/m01`); fairness and equal dealing (`quranic:root_001511:B003/m01`); self-restraint (`quranic:root_000840:B001/m01`); straightness and moderation (`quranic:root_001273:B008/m01`); departure from a relation (`quranic:root_001578:B001/m01`); surpassing the ordinary limit in beauty (`quranic:root_001578:B008/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:3 `نِّصْفَهُ`; 73:6 `أَقْوَمُ`; 73:10 `ٱصْبِرْ/ٱهْجُرْهُمْ/هَجْرًا/جَمِيلًا`
- Synthesis: The beautiful act is a proportioned response. Straightness and fairness prevent withdrawal from becoming either cowardice or aggression, while the lexical “surpassing beauty” intensifies the demanded quality of the departure.

### 8. P1 - Generation, Gestation, and Maturation
- Semantic invariant: Formation proceeds through coupling, conception, containment, growth, care, and eventual strength.
- Surface relation: indirect; 73:4-8 anchors `زِدْ`, `ٱلْقُرْءَانَ`, `ثَقِيلًا`, `نَاشِئَةَ`, `أَشَدُّ`, and `رَبِّكَ`.
- Surprising reach: Night formation and weighty reception activate a complete biological sequence from mating through pregnancy to upbringing and mature strength.

#### Subchannel A. Coupling and conception
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Sexual readiness leads to mounting, intercourse, and rapid conception.
- Active motifs: estrus of a she-camel (`quranic:root_001210:B009/m01`); a stallion mounting females (`quranic:root_000745:B003/m01`); intercourse (`quranic:root_001659:B006/m01`); rapid conception (`quranic:root_001372:B003/m01`); beginning of pregnancy (`quranic:root_001502:B009/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:4 `ٱلْقُرْءَانَ`; 73:5 `نُلْقِى`; 73:6 `نَاشِئَةَ/وَطْـًٔا`; 73:8 `ٱسْمَ`
- Synthesis: These motifs form a sequential reproductive scene rather than a general fertility catalog. Readiness, mounting, union, conception, and the first material onset of pregnancy occupy distinct stages.

#### Subchannel B. Womb, pregnancy, and carried weight
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A womb gathers and contains developing life until pregnancy becomes an embodied burden.
- Active motifs: a womb gathering fetus or blood (`quranic:root_001210:B004/m01`); gathered gestation in the womb (`quranic:root_001211:B003/m01`); pregnancy as heaviness (`quranic:root_000202:B007/m01`); load-bearing (`quranic:root_000644:B001/m01`); creation joined to nurture (`quranic:root_001502:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:1 `ٱلْمُزَّمِّلُ`; 73:4 `ٱلْقُرْءَانَ`; 73:5 `ثَقِيلًا`; 73:6 `نَاشِئَةَ`
- Synthesis: Gathering and carrying are the central operations. The weighty saying and the wrapped bearer are reframed through gestation: reception can be a prolonged containment in which a formed thing grows before emergence.

#### Subchannel C. Upbringing into mature strength
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: New life grows by sustained care until it reaches maturity, firmness, and a middle stage of life.
- Active motifs: youth growing within a people (`quranic:root_001502:B002/m01`); increase and growth (`quranic:root_000657:B001/m01`); nurture and completion by stages (`quranic:root_000532:B002/m01`); a caretaker and raised child (`quranic:root_000532:B005/m01`); recent birth and youth (`quranic:root_000532:B009/m01`); reaching mature strength (`quranic:root_000782:B004/m01`); middle age (`quranic:root_001511:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:3 `نِّصْفَهُ`; 73:4 `زِدْ`; 73:6 `نَاشِئَةَ/أَشَدُّ`; 73:8-9 `رَبِّكَ/رَبُّ`
- Synthesis: Growth is not spontaneous enlargement but staged formation under care. The night’s rising and increase resonate with education and maturation, culminating in strength rather than mere size.

### 9. P1 - Valuation and Provisioning
- Semantic invariant: Resources are assessed, priced, apportioned, and stored so activity can be sustained without either scarcity or waste.
- Surface relation: indirect; 73:3-7 anchors `نِّصْفَهُ`, `ٱنقُصْ`, `زِدْ`, `ثَقِيلًا`, `أَقْوَمُ`, and `سَبْحًا طَوِيلًا`.
- Surprising reach: Night measurement shades into market appraisal, while daytime movement becomes the livelihood that requires capital, provisions, and fair valuation.

#### Subchannel A. Weighing, pricing, and bargaining
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Goods are weighed and assigned value while buyer and seller negotiate increase or reduction.
- Active motifs: known weight (`quranic:root_000202:B004/m01`); a precious weighted object (`quranic:root_000202:B005/m01`); valuation and pricing (`quranic:root_001273:B010/m01`); equal-weight coin (`quranic:root_001273:B015/m01`); competitive bidding (`quranic:root_000657:B004/m01`); asking a lower price (`quranic:root_001542:B004/m01`); equitable dealing (`quranic:root_001511:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:3 `نِّصْفَهُ/ٱنقُصْ`; 73:4 `زِدْ`; 73:5 `ثَقِيلًا`; 73:6 `أَقْوَمُ`
- Synthesis: The same operations that adjust the night form a market scene: weigh, price, bid upward, bargain downward, and settle on an equitable amount. “Heavy” gains the additional sense of valued merchandise.

#### Subchannel B. Means, livelihood, and travel stores
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A household or traveler secures the means that keep body and movement going.
- Active motifs: roaming for livelihood (`quranic:root_000666:B005/m01`); means, wealth, and capacity (`quranic:root_000959:B004/m01`); travel provisions (`quranic:root_000657:B005/m01`); support and subsistence (`quranic:root_001273:B009/m01`); scarcity and poverty (`quranic:root_001251:B001/m01`); need (`quranic:root_000532:B016/m01`); acquisition for oneself (`quranic:root_000018:B010/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:2-3 `قَلِيلًا`; 73:4 `زِدْ`; 73:6 `أَقْوَمُ`; 73:7 `سَبْحًا/طَوِيلًا`; 73:8-9 `رَبِّكَ/رَبُّ/فَٱتَّخِذْهُ`
- Synthesis: Daytime breadth is materially a field of earning and movement. Capacity, acquired stores, and subsistence answer scarcity, while “need and blessing” keeps provision within a relation of dependence rather than autonomous wealth.

### 10. P1 - Concentration, Preservation, and Stored Food
- Semantic invariant: Perishable material is reduced, thickened, dried, melted, mixed, and stored to make concentrated sustenance.
- Surface relation: indirect; 73:3-10 anchors `نِّصْفَهُ`, `ٱلْمَشْرِقِ`, `ٱلْمَغْرِبِ`, `ٱصْبِرْ`, and `جَمِيلًا`.
- Surprising reach: Reducing the night by half resonates with reducing drink by half, while patience and horizon heat open onto preserved travel food.

#### Subchannel A. Reducing and concentrating food
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Liquids and fats are heated, dried, or combined until they become concentrated and durable.
- Active motifs: drink cooked down by half (`quranic:root_001511:B008/m01`); thick syrup or residue used to preserve and repair (`quranic:root_000532:B006/m01`); melted fat (`quranic:root_000260:B005/m01`); sun-dried meat (`quranic:root_000790:B002/m01`); a date-milk-ghee preparation (`quranic:root_001659:B007/m01`); wine or pressed date drink (`quranic:root_001077:B016/m01`); bitter medicinal aloe (`quranic:root_000840:B008/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:3 `نِّصْفَهُ`; 73:6 `وَطْـًٔا`; 73:8-9 `رَبِّكَ/رَبُّ/ٱلْمَشْرِقِ/ٱلْمَغْرِبِ`; 73:10 `ٱصْبِرْ/جَمِيلًا`
- Synthesis: Half-reduction, thickening, melting, sun-drying, and mixing are distinct preservation operations in one kitchen process. The bitter aloe adds medicinal concentration, giving patient endurance a literal taste.

#### Subchannel B. Packing and serving durable stores
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Concentrated provisions are packed into vessels or sacks and set out as a stored food supply.
- Active motifs: travel stores (`quranic:root_000657:B005/m01`); a large storage jar (`quranic:root_001251:B003/m01`); food heaped or laid on a table (`quranic:root_000840:B011/m01`); a food sack and prepared mixture (`quranic:root_001659:B007/m01`); thickened food and repaired skins (`quranic:root_000532:B006/m01`); taking the whole load (`quranic:root_000644:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:1 `ٱلْمُزَّمِّلُ`; 73:2-3 `قَلِيلًا`; 73:4 `زِدْ`; 73:6 `وَطْـًٔا`; 73:8-9 `رَبِّكَ/رَبُّ`; 73:10 `ٱصْبِرْ`
- Synthesis: Processing terminates in storage and service. Jar, sack, preserved skin, heap, and complete load make a compact provisioning scene suited to the traveler activated elsewhere in P1.

### 11. P1 - Binding, Contest, and Release
- Semantic invariant: Force organizes bodies by fastening them, bringing them into direct contest, or deliberately releasing them from engagement.
- Surface relation: indirect; 73:5-10 anchors `ثَقِيلًا`, `أَشَدُّ وَطْـًٔا`, `فَٱتَّخِذْهُ`, `ٱصْبِرْ`, and `ٱهْجُرْهُمْ`.
- Surprising reach: The restrained response at 73:10 stands against a dense latent combat frame of hobbling, wrestling grips, crushing pressure, reprisal, and feud.

#### Subchannel A. Fastening and immobilizing a body
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Rope and fastening points bind an animal or person and restrict movement.
- Active motifs: tying and securing a bond (`quranic:root_000782:B001/m01`); a hobble joining limb to limb (`quranic:root_001578:B007/m01`); a long tether (`quranic:root_000959:B002/m01`); thick rope (`quranic:root_000260:B002/m01`); a gripping handle (`quranic:root_000018:B012/m01`); a stopper sealing an opening (`quranic:root_000840:B018/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:5 `ثَقِيلًا`; 73:6 `أَشَدُّ`; 73:7 `طَوِيلًا`; 73:9 `فَٱتَّخِذْهُ`; 73:10 `ٱصْبِرْ/ٱهْجُرْهُمْ`
- Synthesis: The scene moves from rope to tether, hobble, handle, and stopper, each restricting a different degree of freedom. It supplies a concrete mechanics of restraint beneath the ethical command to restrain the self.

#### Subchannel B. Grappling, crushing, and feud
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Opponents close distance, seize one another, press violently, and perpetuate retaliation.
- Active motifs: wrestling grip (`quranic:root_000018:B011/m01`); resistance and close contest (`quranic:root_001273:B014/m01`); a combat charge (`quranic:root_000782:B003/m01`); crushing seizure (`quranic:root_001659:B003/m01`); captive execution (`quranic:root_000840:B002/m01`); retaliatory killing (`quranic:root_000840:B012/m01`); blood feud (`quranic:root_000959:B008/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:5 `ثَقِيلًا`; 73:6 `أَشَدُّ/وَطْـًٔا/أَقْوَمُ`; 73:7 `طَوِيلًا`; 73:9 `فَٱتَّخِذْهُ`; 73:10 `ٱصْبِرْ`
- Synthesis: Grip, resistance, charge, and pressure form one close-combat sequence, while execution and feud extend its outcome through time. P1’s beautiful withdrawal becomes a refusal to enter precisely this self-renewing mechanism.

### 12. P1 - Acute Bodily Seizure and Sensory Failure
- Semantic invariant: Pressure becomes pathology when it takes hold of the body, distorts perception, and produces tremor, pain, or collapse.
- Surface relation: indirect; 73:2-10 anchors `قَلِيلًا`, `ثَقِيلًا`, `أَشَدُّ`, `ٱلْمَغْرِبِ`, `فَٱتَّخِذْهُ`, and `ٱصْبِرْ`.
- Surprising reach: The bodily cost of “heaviness” is distributed across ear, eye, face, limbs, and involuntary movement.

#### Subchannel A. Face, ear, and eye under impairment
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An affliction takes the body and progressively compromises face, hearing, and sight.
- Active motifs: bodily illness taking hold (`quranic:root_000018:B007/m01`); facial palsy and distortion (`quranic:root_001372:B001/m01`); impaired hearing (`quranic:root_000202:B009/m01`); an intact-looking but blind eye (`quranic:root_001273:B021/m01`); running or swollen eye channels (`quranic:root_001077:B004/m01`); severe bodily disease (`quranic:root_001077:B017/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:2,6 `قُمِ/أَقْوَمُ`; 73:5 `نُلْقِى/ثَقِيلًا`; 73:9 `ٱلْمَغْرِبِ/فَٱتَّخِذْهُ`
- Synthesis: The symptoms occupy linked sensory sites: face alignment fails, hearing grows resistant, and the eye either runs or loses sight. “Taking” and “meeting” become invasive pathological actions rather than neutral contact.

#### Subchannel B. Tremor, pain, and burdened movement
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Fear or anger produces shaking while pain and heaviness obstruct upright movement.
- Active motifs: a tremor from fear or anger (`quranic:root_001251:B005/m01`); pain fixed in an organ (`quranic:root_001273:B019/m01`); bodily heaviness and torpor (`quranic:root_000202:B006/m01`); severe pain or disease (`quranic:root_001077:B017/m01`); an ordeal with no exit (`quranic:root_000840:B006/m01`); harsh stony ground (`quranic:root_000840:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:2-3 `قُمِ/قَلِيلًا`; 73:5 `ثَقِيلًا`; 73:6 `أَقْوَمُ`; 73:9 `ٱلْمَغْرِبِ`; 73:10 `ٱصْبِرْ`
- Synthesis: Tremor and localized pain destabilize the standing body, while torpor and rough ground obstruct recovery of gait. The resulting clinical scene gives bodily specificity to endurance under a severe burden.

### 13. P1 - Devotional Authority and Sustaining Care
- Semantic invariant: Worship is sustained by an authority who owns, teaches, nurtures, maintains, and protects the worshiper’s practice.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:2, 8-9 anchor `قُمِ`, `ٱذْكُرِ ٱسْمَ رَبِّكَ`, `رَّبُّ`, `إِلَٰهَ`, and `وَكِيلًا`.
- Surprising reach: Divine rule is not only sovereignty; it includes pedagogy, repair, maintenance, surety, and the skilled direction of a vessel.

#### Subchannel A. Worship through standing, reading, and remembrance
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A devotee stands, reads, remembers, and declares transcendence in an ordered discipline.
- Active motifs: worship and the object of worship (`quranic:root_000047:B001/m01`); standing in prayer or remembrance (`quranic:root_001273:B002/m01`); devotional reading and study (`quranic:root_001210:B006/m01`); worship through praise and prayer (`quranic:root_000666:B001/m01`); declaring transcendence (`quranic:root_000666:B002/m01`); recollection (`quranic:root_000516:B003/m01`); naming aloud (`quranic:root_000516:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:2,6 `قُمِ/أَقْوَمُ`; 73:4 `ٱلْقُرْءَانَ`; 73:7 `سَبْحًا`; 73:8 `ٱذْكُرِ`; 73:9 `إِلَٰهَ`
- Synthesis: The practice joins posture, text, memory, and praise. Each operation has its own role, but together they form a disciplined devotional scene rather than a list of religious terms.

#### Subchannel B. Sovereign, teacher, caretaker, and guarantor
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Authority sustains dependents by teaching, repairing, governing, and guaranteeing what has been entrusted.
- Active motifs: lordship and ownership (`quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`); staged nurture and completion (`quranic:root_000532:B002/m01`); learned divine instruction (`quranic:root_000532:B003/m01`); care and governance (`quranic:root_001273:B004/m01`); sufficient guardian and guarantor (`quranic:root_001681:B006/m01`); delegated responsibility (`quranic:root_001681:B001/m01`); covenant and protected relation (`quranic:root_000532:B011/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:2,6 `قُمِ/أَقْوَمُ`; 73:8-9 `رَبِّكَ/رَبُّ/وَكِيلًا`
- Synthesis: Authority is rendered as an ecology of care: knowledge guides, nurture develops, governance maintains, covenant binds, and surety protects. This widens the surface title “Lord” into the full set of functions needed to sustain the night discipline.

### 14. P2 - Deferred Appointment and Irreversible Arrival
- Semantic invariant: Delay belongs to a fixed schedule whose promised event ultimately arrives, occurs, and acquires the force of an accomplished act.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:11, 14, 17-18, and 20 anchor `مَهِّلْهُمْ قَلِيلًا`, `يَوْمَ`, `يَوْمًا`, `كَانَ وَعْدُهُ مَفْعُولًا`, and `ءَاخَرُونَ`.
- Surprising reach: Temporal reckoning extends into moon stations, lunar halos, star markers, weekday naming, and the contrast between first, later, and terminal positions.

#### Subchannel A. A short reprieve before an appointed time
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A period of calm delay is granted, but it remains bounded by a specified appointment.
- Active motifs: deliberate delay and absence of haste (`quranic:root_001452:B001/m01`); a small interval (`quranic:root_001251:B001/m01`); postponement to a later time (`quranic:root_000019:B002/m01`); an appointment fixed by time or place (`quranic:root_001662:B003/m01`); a duration of time (`quranic:root_001700:B002/m01`); an event present in time (`quranic:root_001332:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:11 `مَهِّلْهُمْ/قَلِيلًا`; 73:14,17 `يَوْمَ/يَوْمًا`; 73:18 `كَانَ/وَعْدُهُ`; 73:20 `ءَاخَرُونَ/يَكُونُ`
- Synthesis: Delay is not cancellation but a measured opening before an appointment. “Later” and temporal occurrence keep the reprieve inside a schedule whose endpoint remains intact.

#### Subchannel B. Promise becoming an accomplished event
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A verbal commitment advances from expectation through arrival to enacted fact.
- Active motifs: a promise opening expectation (`quranic:root_001662:B001/m01`); threatening promise (`quranic:root_001662:B002/m01`); coming and arrival (`quranic:root_000009:B001/m01`); calamity arriving upon a person (`quranic:root_000009:B011/m01`); return to an outcome or final meaning (`quranic:root_000067:B002/m01`); occurrence in time (`quranic:root_001332:B001/m01`); an enacted deed (`quranic:root_001167:B001/m01`); a calamitous day or event (`quranic:root_001700:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:11 `أُو۟لِى`; 73:14,17 `يَوْمَ/يَوْمًا`; 73:18 `كَانَ/وَعْدُهُ/مَفْعُولًا`; 73:20 `ءَاتُ/يَكُونُ`
- Synthesis: The sequence closes the distance between saying and reality. Arrival supplies the transition, occurrence supplies presence, action realizes it, and outcome names what the event finally becomes or means.

#### Subchannel C. Reading the celestial calendar
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Moon position, halo, stellar mansion, and named days provide observable markers for scheduled time.
- Active motifs: the moon taking successive stations (`quranic:root_000018:B008/m01`); a lunar halo (`quranic:root_001613:B003/m01`); a lunar mansion of three stars (`quranic:root_001096:B006/m01`); a guiding mark or landmark (`quranic:root_001040:B002/m01`); the named day Tuesday (`quranic:root_000203:B006/m01`); daylight as a bounded unit (`quranic:root_001700:B001/m01`); an observable sign promising a future condition (`quranic:root_001662:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:14,17 `يَوْمَ/يَوْمًا`; 73:16,19 `أَخَذْ/ٱتَّخَذَ`; 73:18 `وَعْدُهُ`; 73:20 `ثُلُثَىِ/عَلِمَ/غَفُورٌ`
- Synthesis: These motifs form a practical timekeeping scene: celestial position is observed, marked, grouped, and translated into named units. The promised day is thus pressured by a latent calendar whose signs make an appointment legible.

#### Subchannel D. First, later, forward, and rear positions
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A scheduled sequence distinguishes its beginning and predecessors from later, forward, and rearward positions.
- Active motifs: beginning or first position (`quranic:root_000067:B001/m01`); precedence in age, merit, or movement (`quranic:root_001452:B002/m01`); what comes after the first (`quranic:root_000019:B001/m01`); a rearward position (`quranic:root_000019:B003/m01`); forward advance or precedence (`quranic:root_001207:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:11 `مَهِّلْهُمْ/أُو۟لِى`; 73:20 `ءَاخَرُونَ/تُقَدِّمُوا`
- Synthesis: Temporal order is made spatially legible. First and later establish sequence, precedence and advance establish relative movement, and the rear establishes the opposite position; deferred arrival therefore remains an ordered displacement, not an indefinite pause.

### 15. P2 - Punitive Closure of Movement and Intake
- Semantic invariant: Punishment closes ordinary passages: limbs cannot move, the throat cannot pass food, and heat cannot be escaped.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:12-13 and 16-17 anchor `أَنكَالًا`, `جَحِيمًا`, `طَعَامًا ذَا غُصَّةٍ`, `عَذَابًا أَلِيمًا`, `أَخَذْنَٰهُ`, and `تَتَّقُونَ`.
- Surprising reach: Fetters, a wrestling hold, a neck grip, an unwholesome meal, molten residue, and war-fire form one system of bodily closure rather than separate punishment images.

#### Subchannel A. Binding, capture, and the neck grip
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A body is seized, restrained by iron, and controlled at the neck or throat.
- Active motifs: a shackle that prevents movement (`quranic:root_001554:B001/m01`); arrest and capture (`quranic:root_000018:B003/m01`); wrestling hold (`quranic:root_000018:B011/m01`); taking hold around the neck (`quranic:root_000957:B009/m01`); gripping and crushing the throat (`quranic:root_000934:B012/m01`); direct striking (`quranic:root_000906:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:12 `أَنكَالًا`; 73:13 `طَعَامًا`; 73:16 `أَخَذْنَٰهُ/أَخْذًا`; 73:20 `يَضْرِبُونَ/طَآئِفَةٌ`
