- Synthesis: Restraint proceeds from limb to neck: shackle prevents locomotion, capture removes agency, and the grappling hold concentrates control at the throat. The punitive scene is therefore mechanically continuous.

#### Subchannel B. Food that cannot be swallowed
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A meal enters the mouth but lodges in the throat, turning nourishment into deprivation and pain.
- Active motifs: tasting and taking food (`quranic:root_000934:B001/m01`); obstruction in the throat (`quranic:root_001091:B001/m01`); abstention from food and drink (`quranic:root_000994:B002/m01`); prevention and weaning from a thing (`quranic:root_000994:B003/m01`); painful punishment (`quranic:root_000994:B005/m01`); unwholesome food that cannot be digested (`quranic:root_001619:B003/m01`); felt pain (`quranic:root_000046:B001/m01`); infliction of pain (`quranic:root_000046:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:13 `طَعَامًا/غُصَّةٍ/عَذَابًا/أَلِيمًا`; 73:16 `وَبِيلًا`
- Synthesis: Taste initiates an expected nourishment sequence, but obstruction arrests it. Abstention becomes involuntary, the food proves unwholesome, and the failed passage culminates in inflicted and experienced pain.

#### Subchannel C. Enclosure by fire and molten heat
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Fire, molten matter, and extreme heat surround the punished body.
- Active motifs: blazing infernal fire (`quranic:root_000225:B001/m01`); exposure to fire and its heat (`quranic:root_000879:B001/m01`); molten metal, oily dregs, or pus-like residue (`quranic:root_001452:B003/m01`); punishment by severe pain (`quranic:root_000994:B005/m01`); harmful heaviness (`quranic:root_001619:B002/m01`); clamor of fire and war (`quranic:root_001434:B001/m01`); marching in extreme heat (`quranic:root_001434:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:11 `مَهِّلْهُمْ`; 73:12 `جَحِيمًا`; 73:13 `عَذَابًا`; 73:16 `وَبِيلًا`; 73:20 `صَّلَوٰةَ`; surface anchors unavailable for `quranic:root_001434:B001` and `quranic:root_001434:B002` because `م ع ع` is missing from root coverage
- Synthesis: The scene combines radiant heat, direct burning, molten residue, and suffocating clamor. Even movement through the environment becomes a march inside heat, completing the closure of escape.

### 16. P2 - Failure of Earthly, Celestial, and Hydraulic Containment
- Semantic invariant: Structures that normally support, cover, or channel material lose integrity and release what they hold.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:14 and 18 anchor `تَرْجُفُ ٱلْأَرْضُ وَٱلْجِبَالُ`, `كَثِيبًا مَّهِيلًا`, and `ٱلسَّمَآءُ مُنفَطِرٌ`.
- Surprising reach: Mountain collapse, sky rupture, flood, runoff, and hidden erosion are variants of one containment failure across solid, aerial, and liquid media.

#### Subchannel A. Mountain mass becoming poured sand
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A hard elevated mass shakes, loses resistance, granulates, and pours away.
- Active motifs: violent shaking (`quranic:root_000545:B001/m01`); earth as the lower support (`quranic:root_000025:B001/m01`); a raised solid mountain mass (`quranic:root_000217:B001/m01`); hardness that stops excavation (`quranic:root_000217:B005/m01`); encounter with broad sand (`quranic:root_000217:B011/m01`); gathered dune material (`quranic:root_001285:B001/m01`); loose measure pouring without stability (`quranic:root_001613:B001/m01`); occurrence and transformation in time (`quranic:root_001332:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:14 `تَرْجُفُ/ٱلْأَرْضُ/ٱلْجِبَالُ/كَانَتِ/كَثِيبًا/مَّهِيلًا`
- Synthesis: The process is a phase change in structural behavior: rigid mass begins to oscillate, breaks into aggregate, and flows like unmeasured grain. The branch sequence gives precise mechanics to the surface simile.

#### Subchannel B. The overhead cover splitting open
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: What normally rises above and covers the world tears and exposes the space beneath.
- Active motifs: sky or overhead canopy (`quranic:root_000745:B004/m01`); opening and splitting (`quranic:root_001165:B001/m01`); the lower realm opposite the sky (`quranic:root_000025:B001/m01`); a covering darkness or sea (`quranic:root_001307:B002/m01`); basic covering and concealment (`quranic:root_001307:B001/m01`); an exposed body with no cover from the sky (`quranic:root_000994:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:13 `عَذَابًا`; 73:14 `ٱلْأَرْضُ`; 73:17 `كَفَرْتُمْ`; 73:18 `ٱلسَّمَآءُ/مُنفَطِرٌ`
- Synthesis: Sky and earth establish the vertical enclosure; splitting removes its upper boundary. The covering senses of `ك ف ر` sharpen the reversal: the same lexical field that hides now supplies the cover that can no longer remain closed.

#### Subchannel C. Water exceeding channels and boundaries
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Heavy rain and imported runoff overwhelm the distinction between channel, land, and enclosing wall.
- Active motifs: an encircling flood (`quranic:root_000957:B002/m01`); heavy rain with large drops (`quranic:root_001619:B001/m01`); rain exceeding ordinary need (`quranic:root_000138:B006/m01`); runoff arriving from another land (`quranic:root_000009:B005/m01`); rain hanging between cloud and earth (`quranic:root_000672:B005/m01`); weather striking earth and vegetation (`quranic:root_000906:B013/m01`); a fortified enclosing wall (`quranic:root_000957:B010/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:14,20 `ٱلْأَرْضُ/أَرْضِ`; 73:16 `وَبِيلًا`; 73:19-20 `سَبِيلًا/سَبِيلِ`; 73:20 `ءَاتُ/يَبْتَغُونَ/يَضْرِبُونَ/طَآئِفَةٌ`
- Synthesis: Rain descends, runoff arrives laterally, and flood circles the site until ordinary boundaries fail. The enclosing wall supplies the expected defense whose insufficiency makes the inundation a true collapse scene.

#### Subchannel D. Managed flow as the failed baseline
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Water is ordinarily directed through a channel, gathered in a basin, retained at a source, and released into a river.
- Active motifs: a watercourse and the directing of its flow (`quranic:root_000009:B004/m01`); a basin that retains water (`quranic:root_000018:B006/m01`); abundant well or sea water (`quranic:root_001040:B005/m01`); gathered water (`quranic:root_000532:B013/m01`); a river (`quranic:root_001559:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:16,19 `أَخَذْنَٰهُ/ٱتَّخَذَ`; 73:19-20 `رَبِّهِ/رَبَّكَ`; 73:20 `ءَاتُ/عَلِمَ/ٱلنَّهَارِ`
- Synthesis: Channel, basin, source, store, and river define a controlled hydraulic system. That ordinary sequence supplies the baseline against which excess runoff and rupture become failures of containment rather than undifferentiated water imagery.

### 17. P2 - Message Tested by Testimony and Enactment
- Semantic invariant: A communicated claim is tested by witness, enacted fact, and the quality of its recipients’ response.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:11, 15-16, and 18-19 anchor `ٱلْمُكَذِّبِينَ`, `رَسُولًا شَٰهِدًا`, `عَصَىٰ ... ٱلرَّسُولَ`, `وَعْدُهُ مَفْعُولًا`, and `تَذْكِرَةٌ`.
- Surprising reach: Falsehood appears not only as lying but as a failed charge, deceptive cloth, rumor-induced panic, and fabricated composition.

#### Subchannel A. Envoy, carried message, and witness
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: An envoy arrives bearing a message, observes its recipients, and provides knowledgeable testimony.
- Active motifs: dispatch and release (`quranic:root_000563:B001/m01`); messenger and carried message (`quranic:root_000563:B002/m01`); presence with direct observation (`quranic:root_000822:B001/m01`); testimony grounded in knowledge (`quranic:root_000822:B002/m01`); the tongue as a witness (`quranic:root_000822:B005/m01`); a sign that bears witness to time or condition (`quranic:root_000822:B008/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:15 `أَرْسَلْنَا/رَسُولًا/شَٰهِدًا`; 73:16 `ٱلرَّسُولَ`
- Synthesis: Dispatch supplies the sender-recipient relation, the messenger carries the content, presence supplies observation, and testimony converts observation into accountable statement. Tongue and external marker extend witnessing from person to instrument and sign.

#### Subchannel B. Denial, rumor, and deceptive representation
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Recipients reject the message while false representations circulate and generate disturbance.
- Active motifs: falsehood opposed to truth (`quranic:root_001290:B001/m01`); attributing falsehood to a speaker (`quranic:root_001290:B002/m01`); a deceptive dyed or patterned cloth (`quranic:root_001290:B009/m01`); denial and concealment of truth (`quranic:root_001307:B003/m01`); alarming or false rumor (`quranic:root_000545:B003/m01`); fabricated discourse made without a prior model (`quranic:root_001167:B004/m01`); disobedience to authority (`quranic:root_001022:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:11 `ٱلْمُكَذِّبِينَ`; 73:14 `تَرْجُفُ`; 73:16 `عَصَىٰ`; 73:17 `كَفَرْتُمْ`; 73:18 `مَفْعُولًا`
- Synthesis: The scene distinguishes several failures of representation: direct lying, accusing another of lying, visual deception, fabricated composition, and rumor. Disobedience is the behavioral outcome when that representational refusal is directed at an envoy.

#### Subchannel C. Commitment verified by action
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A promise is judged true when a corresponding act occurs and becomes observable fact.
- Active motifs: verbal commitment (`quranic:root_001662:B001/m01`); mutual commitment (`quranic:root_001662:B004/m01`); an enacted deed (`quranic:root_001167:B001/m01`); occurrence in time (`quranic:root_001332:B001/m01`); knowledgeable disclosure (`quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`); finding an object as present (`quranic:root_001626:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:14,18,20 `كَانَتِ/كَانَ/يَكُونُ`; 73:18 `وَعْدُهُ/مَفْعُولًا`; 73:20 `يَعْلَمُ/عَلِمَ/تَجِدُوهُ`
- Synthesis: Promise and action are not merged: promise establishes the claim, occurrence gives it temporal presence, knowledge recognizes it, and finding supplies experiential verification. The “fulfilled promise” is therefore a completed evidentiary chain.

#### Subchannel D. Answer, assent, and unstable alignment
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A recipient answers affirmatively, then either embodies submission or merely aligns with whichever side prevails.
- Active motifs: saying yes in confirmation or promise (`quranic:root_001525:B004/m01`); answering a call (`quranic:root_001573:B003/m01`); bodily gesture of submission (`quranic:root_001307:B014/m01`); submissive yielding (`quranic:root_001332:B004/m01`); siding with the victor without a settled position (`quranic:root_001434:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:11 `ٱلنَّعْمَةِ`; 73:14,18,20 `كَانَتِ/كَانَ/يَكُونُ`; 73:17 `كَفَرْتُمْ`; surface anchor unavailable for `quranic:root_001573:B003` because `ه ا ء` is missing from root coverage, and for `quranic:root_001434:B005` because `م ع ع` is missing from root coverage
- Synthesis: Verbal yes, responsive answer, bodily deference, and inward yielding can align, but the opportunistic partisan exposes their possible separation. Recipient response is therefore tested not by assent alone but by whether word, posture, and durable position correspond.

### 18. P2 - Covering, Protection, and Exposure
- Semantic invariant: A covering may preserve what is within, conceal what should be known, or fail and expose its contents.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:17-18 and 20 anchor `تَتَّقُونَ`, `كَفَرْتُمْ`, `ٱلسَّمَآءُ مُنفَطِرٌ`, and `غَفُورٌ`.
- Surprising reach: Protective barrier, fruit casing, eye film, garment, roof edge, forgiveness, disbelief, and sky cover all turn on the ethics and mechanics of concealment.

#### Subchannel A. Protective barriers
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A vulnerable object is placed behind a barrier, casing, wall, or film that intercepts harm.
- Active motifs: a barrier against damage (`quranic:root_001677:B001/m01`); placing oneself in protection (`quranic:root_001677:B002/m01`); a fortified enclosing wall (`quranic:root_000957:B010/m01`); fruit casing (`quranic:root_001307:B010/m01`); a woven film over the eye (`quranic:root_000672:B010/m01`); covering that preserves an object (`quranic:root_001096:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:17 `تَتَّقُونَ/كَفَرْتُمْ`; 73:19-20 `سَبِيلًا/سَبِيلِ/غَفُورٌ/طَآئِفَةٌ`
- Synthesis: The barriers differ in scale but share one operation: interpose a layer between a vulnerable interior and damaging exterior. Piety is materialized as deliberately entering such a protective arrangement.

#### Subchannel B. Concealing truth and covering fault
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Covering either suppresses a recognized truth or shields a wrongdoer from the consequence of fault.
- Active motifs: basic covering and concealment (`quranic:root_001307:B001/m01`); denial that hides truth (`quranic:root_001307:B003/m01`); covering or denying a benefit (`quranic:root_001307:B004/m01`); covering sin and protecting its bearer from its effect (`quranic:root_001096:B002/m01`); removing an offense as though covered (`quranic:root_001307:B009/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:17 `كَفَرْتُمْ`; 73:20 `ٱسْتَغْفِرُوا/غَفُورٌ`
- Synthesis: The shared action of covering yields opposed moral relations. Denial uses a cover to suppress truth or benefit; forgiveness uses it to contain fault and prevent its destructive consequence.

#### Subchannel C. Roof, drape, and sky torn open
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Overhead and hanging coverings are lowered or removed until the protected interior stands exposed.
- Active motifs: sky as overhead canopy (`quranic:root_000745:B004/m01`); splitting open (`quranic:root_001165:B001/m01`); roof terrace without a parapet (`quranic:root_000015:B003/m01`); exposure directly beneath the sky (`quranic:root_000994:B004/m01`); lowering a hanging cover (`quranic:root_000672:B004/m01`); draping a garment (`quranic:root_001163:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:13 `عَذَابًا`; 73:18 `ٱلسَّمَآءُ/مُنفَطِرٌ`; 73:19-20 `سَبِيلًا/سَبِيلِ/أَجْرًا/فَضْلِ`
- Synthesis: Canopy, parapet, and drape define protected edges; split, exposure, and lowering undo them. The ruptured sky becomes the largest instance of a familiar architectural failure.

### 19. P2 - Compressed Life Cycle
- Semantic invariant: Birth, growth, maturation, and aging can be accelerated until distant life stages collapse into one event.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:17 anchors `يَجْعَلُ ٱلْوِلْدَٰنَ شِيبًا`, with 73:13, 15, and 20 supplying `عَذَابًا`, `شَٰهِدًا`, `مَرْضَىٰ`, `زَّكَوٰةَ`, and `رَّحِيمٌ`.
- Surprising reach: The child-to-grey transformation is supported by a detailed reproductive ledger of womb, afterbirth, childbirth blood, near delivery, tooth eruption, and derived new life.

#### Subchannel A. Womb, delivery, and afterbirth
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Gestation approaches term, the child emerges, and bodily traces of delivery remain.
- Active motifs: womb as the vessel of offspring (`quranic:root_000552:B003/m01`); labor and placing down the child (`quranic:root_001683:B003/m01`); a newborn child (`quranic:root_001683:B004/m01`); afterbirth and womb discharge (`quranic:root_000994:B009/m01`); birth fluid or sign of maturity (`quranic:root_000822:B006/m01`); childbirth and postpartum blood (`quranic:root_001533:B005/m01`); nearing delivery (`quranic:root_000493:B004/m01`); postpartum uterine pain (`quranic:root_000552:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:13 `عَذَابًا`; 73:15 `شَٰهِدًا`; 73:17 `ٱلْوِلْدَٰنَ`; 73:20 `أَدْنَىٰ/أَنفُسِكُم/رَّحِيمٌ`
- Synthesis: The motifs occupy successive roles in one birth scene: containing organ, approaching term, delivery, newborn, fluid trace, blood, and after-pain. This gives the surface’s children an extended biological origin rather than treating age as an isolated image.

#### Subchannel B. Childhood turning immediately to old age
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A young dependent is transformed into a grey-haired figure without traversing ordinary duration.
- Active motifs: offspring and child (`quranic:root_001683:B001/m01`); a recently born child (`quranic:root_001683:B004/m01`); white hair and aging (`quranic:root_000833:B001/m01`); an old man defined by looking back on youth (`quranic:root_001332:B005/m01`); a calamitous day (`quranic:root_001700:B003/m01`); transforming a person into a new state (`quranic:root_000248:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:14,18,20 `كَانَتِ/كَانَ/يَكُونُ`; 73:17 `يَوْمًا/يَجْعَلُ/ٱلْوِلْدَٰنَ/شِيبًا`
- Synthesis: The process is temporal compression expressed as transformation. Newborn status and retrospective old age are forced into direct adjacency by the calamitous day.

#### Subchannel C. Emergence, growth, and bodily markers
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A newly created thing emerges, grows, and displays visible markers of development.
- Active motifs: generation of one thing from another (`quranic:root_001683:B005/m01`); creating and beginning (`quranic:root_001165:B002/m01`); tooth eruption (`quranic:root_001165:B007/m01`); growth and increase (`quranic:root_000637:B001/m01`); yield and production (`quranic:root_000009:B007/m01`); making or creating (`quranic:root_000248:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:17 `يَجْعَلُ/ٱلْوِلْدَٰنَ`; 73:18 `مُنفَطِرٌ`; 73:20 `ءَاتُ/زَّكَوٰةَ`
- Synthesis: Creation is followed by derived existence, organic increase, productive yield, and the visible eruption of a tooth. The normal sequence provides the baseline that the sudden greying violently overrides.

#### Subchannel D. Lineage, household, and age cohort
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A child enters a network of parents, kin, household dependents, caretakers, and age peers.
- Active motifs: parents (`quranic:root_001683:B002/m01`); kinship and family bond (`quranic:root_000552:B002/m01`); household or attached followers (`quranic:root_000067:B003/m01`); a caretaker and stepchild relation (`quranic:root_000532:B005/m01`); an age peer (`quranic:root_001683:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:11 `أُو۟لِى`; 73:17 `ٱلْوِلْدَٰنَ`; 73:19-20 `رَبِّهِ/رَبَّكَ`; 73:20 `رَّحِيمٌ`
- Synthesis: Birth does not produce an isolated body. It inserts the child into vertical descent, lateral kinship, domestic care, and a same-age cohort; the sudden greying therefore also disrupts the relational positions by which age is ordinarily recognized.

#### Subchannel E. Reproductive interval and recurrence
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Menstruation alternates with purity, while the interval itself supplies a recurring unit for waiting and reproductive time.
- Active motifs: menstrual phase and its completion (`quranic:root_001210:B003/m01`); interval of purity and waiting between phases (`quranic:root_001210:B003/m02`); the appointed recurrence of menstruation and purity (`quranic:root_001211:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:20 `ٱقْرَءُوا/قُرْءَانِ`
- Synthesis: The branches distinguish bodily phase, intervening interval, and cyclic return. This recurrent reproductive clock supplies the ordinary temporal baseline from which gestation begins and against which the surah’s immediate child-to-grey transformation appears radically compressed.

### 20. P2 - Volition, Compulsion, and Viable Capacity
- Semantic invariant: Action can arise from chosen direction, external coercion, or a capacity calibrated to what is actually possible.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:17, 19-20 anchor `تَتَّقُونَ إِن كَفَرْتُمْ`, `فَمَن شَآءَ ٱتَّخَذَ ... سَبِيلًا`, and `فَٱقْرَءُوا مَا تَيَسَّرَ`.
- Surprising reach: Choice is pressured by lexical scenes of forcing, deterrent restraint, suitability, preparation, illness, and eased passage.

#### Subchannel A. Will selecting a way
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A person forms a will, takes a route as their own, and directs it toward a governing end.
- Active motifs: will directed toward a thing (`quranic:root_000831:B002/m01`); volition (`quranic:root_000832:B001/m01`); adopting something for oneself (`quranic:root_000018:B010/m01`); a traversable way (`quranic:root_000672:B001/m01`); lordship as governing endpoint (`quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`); reminder restoring an option to awareness (`quranic:root_000516:B009/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:19 `تَذْكِرَةٌ/شَآءَ/ٱتَّخَذَ/رَبِّهِ/سَبِيلًا`
- Synthesis: The reminder makes the option cognitively present, will selects it, taking establishes personal commitment, and the path converts decision into directed movement.

#### Subchannel B. Being carried, forced, or deterred
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An external agent moves another toward an act or blocks movement through compulsion and exemplary restraint.
- Active motifs: carrying or compelling a person toward an affair (`quranic:root_000831:B003/m01`); forcing someone into an act (`quranic:root_000217:B010/m01`); coercing an obedient person into disobedience (`quranic:root_001307:B007/m01`); transforming someone’s state (`quranic:root_000248:B002/m01`); a shackle that prevents movement (`quranic:root_001554:B001/m01`); punishment that deters observers (`quranic:root_001554:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:12 `أَنكَالًا`; 73:14 `ٱلْجِبَالُ`; 73:17 `كَفَرْتُمْ/يَجْعَلُ`; 73:19 `شَآءَ`
- Synthesis: Compulsion and deterrence are distinct operations: one pushes a body toward an act, the other stops recurrence by displaying restraint and consequence. Together they define the non-voluntary alternatives against which chosen path-taking is read.

#### Subchannel C. Capacity adjusted to ease and suitability
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A task is planned to fit available strength, health, and circumstances so it can be performed without collapse.
- Active motifs: effective ability and mastery (`quranic:root_001205:B003/m01`); a thing fitting its measure (`quranic:root_001205:B006/m01`); ease after difficulty (`quranic:root_001694:B001/m01`); what is suitable or readily possible (`quranic:root_000138:B002/m01`); an approach that prepares the affair (`quranic:root_000009:B003/m01`); weakened action and short movement (`quranic:root_001415:B004/m01`); suitability to one’s condition (`quranic:root_000637:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:20 `يُقَدِّرُ/تَيَسَّرَ/مَرْضَىٰ/يَبْتَغُونَ/ءَاتُوا/زَّكَوٰةَ`
- Synthesis: Capacity is relational, not absolute. Measure, suitability, preparation, and ease adapt the operation to impaired movement and changing circumstance, explaining how obligation can remain real while its quantity changes.

### 21. P2 - Paths of Approach, Livelihood, and Combat
- Semantic invariant: A way becomes meaningful through its destination and the kind of effort conducted along it.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:19-20 anchor `ٱتَّخَذَ ... سَبِيلًا`, `يَضْرِبُونَ فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ يَبْتَغُونَ`, and `يُقَٰتِلُونَ فِى سَبِيلِ ٱللَّهِ`.
- Surprising reach: The same route system supports spiritual approach, commerce, scouting, return from travel, and military advance, with landmarks and lateral choices preventing “way” from remaining abstract.

#### Subchannel A. Route, landmark, and directed approach
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A traveler identifies a road by its markers, establishes direction, and approaches a destination.
- Active motifs: a road or means of access (`quranic:root_000672:B001/m01`); a guiding landmark (`quranic:root_001040:B002/m01`); a traveled thoroughfare and terminal line (`quranic:root_000009:B010/m01`); nearness and approach (`quranic:root_000493:B001/m01`); front or leading edge (`quranic:root_001207:B007/m01`); presence at the destination (`quranic:root_001052:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:19 `ٱتَّخَذَ/رَبِّهِ/سَبِيلًا`; 73:20 `ءَاتُوا/أَدْنَىٰ/عَلِمَ/عِندَ/تُقَدِّمُوا`
- Synthesis: Road, landmark, forepart, nearness, and arrival assign concrete roles to path-taking. Direction is not merely moral orientation; it is a navigable approach whose progress can be observed.

#### Subchannel B. Commercial travel in search of surplus
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Travelers cross the land to seek resources, conduct partnership trade, and return with gain.
- Active motifs: travel through the land for livelihood (`quranic:root_000906:B002/m01`); seeking a desired resource (`quranic:root_000138:B001/m01`); surplus remaining after need (`quranic:root_001163:B001/m01`); non-obligatory gift or benefit (`quranic:root_001163:B003/m01`); commercial partnership (`quranic:root_000906:B016/m01`); capital entrusted for trade (`quranic:root_001217:B004/m01`); wealth and means (`quranic:root_001626:B003/m01`); return from travel (`quranic:root_001207:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:20 `يَضْرِبُونَ/فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ/يَبْتَغُونَ/فَضْلِ/تَجِدُوهُ/تُقَدِّمُوا`
- Synthesis: The route supports a full commercial process: search, travel, pooled capital, productive effort, surplus, and return. “Seeking bounty” is thereby grounded in a recognizable economy rather than generalized striving.

#### Subchannel C. Advancing along a battle route
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A force scouts ahead, mobilizes, advances with courage, and engages an opposing side.
- Active motifs: reciprocal combat (`quranic:root_001200:B011/m01`); incitement toward battle (`quranic:root_000906:B017/m01`); advance scouts (`quranic:root_000138:B008/m01`); preparation for war (`quranic:root_000545:B004/m01`); courageous advance (`quranic:root_001207:B006/m01`); clamor of fighters and fire (`quranic:root_001434:B001/m01`); a traversable way (`quranic:root_000672:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:14 `تَرْجُفُ`; 73:19-20 `سَبِيلًا/يَضْرِبُونَ/يَبْتَغُونَ/يُقَٰتِلُونَ/سَبِيلِ`; surface anchor unavailable for `quranic:root_001434:B001` because `م ع ع` is missing from root coverage
- Synthesis: Scouting precedes mobilization, incitement initiates forward motion, courage sustains it, and reciprocal combat supplies the terminal encounter. The path is the organizing corridor for the whole sequence.

#### Subchannel D. Lateral deviation and positional choice
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A traveler can refuse the direct line, separate toward one side, pass laterally, or choose a forward or rearward position.
- Active motifs: refusal and deviation from a true course (`quranic:root_001052:B001/m01`); side-separation (`quranic:root_001052:B002/m01`); passing a place to one side (`quranic:root_001217:B002/m01`); taking the left side (`quranic:root_001694:B004/m01`); the rear or later position (`quranic:root_000019:B003/m01`); forward advance (`quranic:root_001207:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:20 `عِندَ/قَرْضًا/تَيَسَّرَ/ءَاخَرُونَ/تُقَدِّمُوا`
- Synthesis: A path includes crosswise decisions as well as forward progress. Deviation, separation, bypass, leftward movement, rear position, and advance map the alternatives by which an intended approach can be maintained or lost.

### 22. P2 - Measured Contribution and Returned Value
- Semantic invariant: A contribution is first apportioned, then transferred, and finally recovered as value, benefit, or reward.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:20 anchors `ثُلُثَىِ ٱلَّيْلِ وَنِصْفَهُ وَثُلُثَهُ`, `يُقَدِّرُ`, `تُحْصُوهُ`, `أَقْرِضُوا ... قَرْضًا حَسَنًا`, `تُقَدِّمُوا`, `تَجِدُوهُ`, and `أَعْظَمَ أَجْرًا`.
- Surprising reach: Night fractions and devotional giving share the same operational grammar of amount, ledger, advance, loan, equivalent return, and multiplied value.

#### Subchannel A. Portioning and counting the night
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: A duration is divided into exact fractions, estimated, counted, and adjusted to a manageable amount.
- Active motifs: the number three (`quranic:root_000203:B001/m01`); a third portion (`quranic:root_000203:B002/m01`); one half (`quranic:root_001511:B001/m01`); reaching a midpoint (`quranic:root_001511:B002/m01`); specified quantity and limit (`quranic:root_001205:B001/m01`); planning by calculation (`quranic:root_001205:B005/m01`); exhaustive counting (`quranic:root_000332:B002/m01`); equal weight or moderate quantity (`quranic:root_001273:B015/m01`); a small amount (`quranic:root_001251:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:11 `قَلِيلًا`; 73:20 `ثُلُثَىِ/نِصْفَهُ/ثُلُثَهُ/يُقَدِّرُ/تُحْصُوهُ/تَقُومُ`
- Synthesis: Number names identify the portions, midpoint locates them, measure sets their limit, and calculation attempts an exact ledger. Fewness and moderate weight allow the same system to reduce the burden when exhaustive counting fails.

#### Subchannel B. Loan, capital, and reciprocal return
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Value is cut from one holder, transferred as capital or loan, and expected to return in equivalent or productive form.
- Active motifs: a loan or good deed whose return is sought (`quranic:root_001217:B003/m01`); capital entrusted for commerce (`quranic:root_001217:B004/m01`); reciprocal recompense (`quranic:root_001217:B005/m01`); commercial partnership (`quranic:root_000906:B016/m01`); giving or transferring (`quranic:root_000009:B002/m01`); wage or reward for work (`quranic:root_000015:B001/m01`); purification and due giving (`quranic:root_000637:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:20 `يَضْرِبُونَ/ءَاتُوا/زَّكَوٰةَ/أَقْرِضُوا/قَرْضًا/أَجْرًا`
- Synthesis: Loan and commercial capital share transfer but differ in contract; reciprocal recompense links both to eventual return. Purifying gift presses the scene beyond profit by making some transfers valuable precisely as relinquishment.

#### Subchannel C. Advancing a deed and finding it enlarged
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A good act is sent ahead, later found present, and recognized as superior reward.
- Active motifs: prior merit and benefaction (`quranic:root_001207:B002/m01`); deliberately directing something forward (`quranic:root_001207:B010/m01`); finding what was sought (`quranic:root_001626:B001/m01`); beneficial good (`quranic:root_000452:B001/m01`); selected excellence (`quranic:root_000452:B002/m01`); magnitude and increased value (`quranic:root_001029:B001/m01`); reward for work (`quranic:root_000015:B001/m01`); beneficent action beyond bare justice (`quranic:root_000323:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:20 `حَسَنًا/تُقَدِّمُوا/خَيْرٍ/تَجِدُوهُ/خَيْرًا/أَعْظَمَ/أَجْرًا`
- Synthesis: The deed is treated as an advance placement rather than a vanished past act. Finding establishes persistence, goodness establishes quality, and magnitude describes the enlarged value of the returned result.

### 23. P2 - Illness, Accommodation, and Restorative Care
- Semantic invariant: When health or operative strength fails, care changes the task’s conditions and opens a path from constriction toward relief.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:20 anchors `عَلِمَ أَن سَيَكُونُ مِنكُم مَّرْضَىٰ`, `تَيَسَّرَ`, `تَابَ عَلَيْكُمْ`, `ٱسْتَغْفِرُوا`, `غَفُورٌ`, and `رَّحِيمٌ`.
- Surprising reach: Accommodation is materialized through nursing, relapse, infected wounds, reduced movement, breathing room, and a measure fitted to the patient.

#### Subchannel A. Illness progressing under care
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A body leaves equilibrium, movement weakens, a wound worsens or relapses, and an attendant works toward recovery.
- Active motifs: departure from bodily health (`quranic:root_001415:B001/m01`); tending a patient (`quranic:root_001415:B003/m01`); weakened action and curtailed movement (`quranic:root_001415:B004/m01`); dimness and loss of clarity (`quranic:root_001415:B005/m01`); illness taking the body (`quranic:root_000018:B007/m01`); relapse of illness or wound (`quranic:root_001096:B004/m01`); a suppurating sore (`quranic:root_000025:B011/m01`); a wound swelling toward corruption (`quranic:root_000138:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:14,20 `ٱلْأَرْضُ/أَرْضِ`; 73:16,19 `أَخَذْنَٰهُ/ٱتَّخَذَ`; 73:20 `مَرْضَىٰ/يَبْتَغُونَ/غَفُورٌ`
- Synthesis: The clinical scene distinguishes disease, impaired function, local infection, relapse, and caregiving. Care is an active counter-process, not merely sympathy for an already named patient.

#### Subchannel B. Constriction opening into relief
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A narrowed obligation is eased, the distressed person receives breathing room, and fault is covered through mercy.
- Active motifs: ease and readiness after difficulty (`quranic:root_001694:B001/m01`); material and practical breadth (`quranic:root_001694:B003/m01`); release from distress (`quranic:root_001533:B002/m01`); mercy and tender care (`quranic:root_000552:B001/m01`); forgiveness shielding from consequence (`quranic:root_001096:B002/m01`); inviting return and repentance (`quranic:root_000189:B003/m01`); constricting an amount (`quranic:root_001205:B004/m01`); fitting a thing to its measure (`quranic:root_001205:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:20 `يُقَدِّرُ/تَابَ/تَيَسَّرَ/مَرْضَىٰ/ٱسْتَغْفِرُوا/غَفُورٌ/رَّحِيمٌ`
- Synthesis: Relief reverses constriction without dissolving measure. Breath, breadth, mercy, return, and forgiveness define distinct stages by which an unmanageable demand becomes a viable practice.

### 24. P2 - Ritual Ordering and Textual Access
- Semantic invariant: Devotion is made repeatable by joining accessible text, bodily maintenance, prayer, purification, and giving.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:19-20 anchor `تَذْكِرَةٌ`, `فَٱقْرَءُوا مَا تَيَسَّرَ`, `أَقِيمُوا ٱلصَّلَوٰةَ`, `ءَاتُوا ٱلزَّكَوٰةَ`, and `أَقْرِضُوا`.
- Surprising reach: Reading is both collection and study; prayer is both formal worship and merciful blessing; almsgiving is simultaneously growth, purification, transfer, and productive advance.

#### Subchannel A. Text gathered, learned, and made accessible
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Scene or process: Verbal material is collected into a readable whole, studied, remembered, and adjusted to what can be managed.
- Active motifs: gathering parts (`quranic:root_001210:B001/m01`); reading and recitation (`quranic:root_001210:B002/m01`); collected wording (`quranic:root_001211:B001/m01`); devotional study (`quranic:root_001210:B006/m01`); ease and readiness (`quranic:root_001694:B001/m01`); reminder restoring presence (`quranic:root_000516:B009/m01`); disclosed knowledge (`quranic:root_001040:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:19 `تَذْكِرَةٌ`; 73:20 `عَلِمَ/ٱقْرَءُوا/قُرْءَانِ/تَيَسَّرَ`
- Synthesis: Collection makes a textual object, reading activates it, study develops competence, reminder restores it to attention, and ease adjusts access. The sequence explains how textual obligation survives changing capacity.

#### Subchannel B. Standing and maintaining prayer
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A worshiper establishes a bodily and institutional practice that is maintained over time.
- Active motifs: bodily standing in prayer (`quranic:root_001273:B002/m01`); maintenance, care, and governance (`quranic:root_001273:B004/m01`); prescribed prayer (`quranic:root_000879:B003/m01`); prayer as blessing and mercy (`quranic:root_000879:B002/m01`); places of worship (`quranic:root_000879:B007/m01`); worship and the worshiped one (`quranic:root_000047:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:20 `تَقُومُ/أَقِيمُوا/ٱلصَّلَوٰةَ/ٱللَّهِ`
- Synthesis: Establishing prayer joins posture to maintenance: one stands, sets the practice upright, and keeps it in force. Blessing gives the practice an outward relational effect, while the worship place supplies its communal setting.

#### Subchannel C. Purifying transfer
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Value leaves the giver as alms or loan and becomes purification, growth, and beneficent action.
- Active motifs: giving and transfer (`quranic:root_000009:B002/m01`); growth and increase (`quranic:root_000637:B001/m01`); purification and moral soundness (`quranic:root_000637:B002/m01`); a good loan whose return is sought (`quranic:root_001217:B003/m01`); beneficent action (`quranic:root_000323:B002/m01`); generous gift beyond obligation (`quranic:root_001163:B003/m01`); mercy toward another (`quranic:root_000552:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:20 `ءَاتُوا/زَّكَوٰةَ/أَقْرِضُوا/قَرْضًا/حَسَنًا/فَضْلِ/رَّحِيمٌ`
- Synthesis: Transfer is productive without being reduced to profit. It cleans the giver, supports growth, benefits the recipient, and establishes a return relation whose quality is defined by goodness and mercy.

### 25. P2 - Provision Ecology
- Semantic invariant: Provision moves through land, rain, crop, livestock, processing, and social distribution.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:11, 13, and 20 anchor `أُو۟لِى ٱلنَّعْمَةِ`, `طَعَامًا`, `فِى ٱلْأَرْضِ`, `يَبْتَغُونَ مِن فَضْلِ ٱللَّهِ`, `ءَاتُوا ٱلزَّكَوٰةَ`, and `خَيْرٍ`.
- Surprising reach: The bundle assembles both a crop cycle and a dairy herd cycle beneath the surah’s contrast between luxury, punitive food, livelihood, and charitable giving.

#### Subchannel A. Rain-fed crop and yield
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Rain reaches fertile ground, seed is covered, shoots form ears, fruit ripens, and yield is gathered.
- Active motifs: fertile productive land (`quranic:root_000025:B002/m01`); covering seed with earth (`quranic:root_001307:B008/m01`); an ear of grain (`quranic:root_000672:B008/m01`); organic growth (`quranic:root_000637:B001/m01`); crop and fruit yield (`quranic:root_000009:B007/m01`); fruit reaching maturity (`quranic:root_000934:B005/m01`); rain exceeding ordinary need (`quranic:root_000138:B006/m01`); a river channel through land (`quranic:root_001559:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:13 `طَعَامًا`; 73:14,20 `ٱلْأَرْضُ/أَرْضِ`; 73:17 `كَفَرْتُمْ`; 73:19-20 `سَبِيلًا/سَبِيلِ`; 73:20 `ءَاتُوا/زَّكَوٰةَ/يَبْتَغُونَ/ٱلنَّهَارَ`
- Synthesis: Water, soil, seed cover, stalk, ripening, growth, and yield form a complete agricultural process. The same earth crossed by traders is also the productive substrate of the bounty they seek.

#### Subchannel B. Herd, milk, and animal nourishment
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Livestock reproduce, fill the udder, release milk, gain fat, and ruminate.
- Active motifs: livestock wealth (`quranic:root_001525:B005/m01`); a she-camel filling three vessels or milking from three teats (`quranic:root_000203:B005/m01`); abundant milk and offspring (`quranic:root_001694:B006/m01`); continuous milk flow (`quranic:root_000563:B006/m01`); fat in animal flesh and marrow (`quranic:root_000934:B007/m01`); a camel chewing cud (`quranic:root_001217:B008/m01`); milk failing after appearing durable (`quranic:root_001290:B006/m01`); mating drive in sheep (`quranic:root_001619:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:11 `ٱلنَّعْمَةِ`; 73:13 `طَعَامًا`; 73:15-16 `أَرْسَلْنَا/ٱلرَّسُولَ/وَبِيلًا`; 73:20 `ثُلُثَىِ/تَيَسَّرَ/قَرْضًا`
- Synthesis: Reproduction initiates a dairy chain whose products are milk, fat, and sustained animal nourishment. The failed-milk motif marks the system’s vulnerability, contrasting real provision with an appearance that does not last.

#### Subchannel C. Food, livelihood, and generous distribution
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Provision is acquired as livelihood, served to others, and redistributed as surplus or gift.
- Active motifs: feeding another and responding to need (`quranic:root_000934:B002/m01`); food as livelihood and good condition (`quranic:root_000934:B004/m01`); surplus after need (`quranic:root_001163:B001/m01`); generous gift (`quranic:root_001163:B003/m01`); generosity and donation (`quranic:root_000452:B005/m01`); wealth and practical means (`quranic:root_001626:B003/m01`); giving (`quranic:root_000009:B002/m01`); blessing and good condition (`quranic:root_001525:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:11 `ٱلنَّعْمَةِ`; 73:13 `طَعَامًا`; 73:20 `ءَاتُوا/يَبْتَغُونَ/فَضْلِ/خَيْرٍ/تَجِدُوهُ`
- Synthesis: Acquisition is only the first stage. Livelihood produces surplus, surplus enables feeding and gift, and giving circulates provision back through the social field.

### 26. P2 - Hearth and Meal Preparation
- Semantic invariant: A meal is made by assembling a hearth, applying heat, cutting material, and controlling concentration.
- Surface relation: indirect; 73:11-13 and 20 anchor `مَهِّلْهُمْ`, `جَحِيمًا`, `طَعَامًا`, `عَذَابًا`, `ثُلُثَهُ`, `أَنفُسِكُم`, and `يُقَٰتِلُونَ`.
- Surprising reach: A pot, third hearthstone, lifting cloth, cut meat, fire, hastily baked dough, reduced drink, and measured sips form an ordinary kitchen whose operations are inverted by the punitive meal.

#### Subchannel A. Building and working the hearth
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A pot is set on three supports, heated, handled with a cloth, and supplied with cut food.
- Active motifs: cooking pot and its contents (`quranic:root_001205:B007/m01`); the third hearthstone completing a support (`quranic:root_000203:B007/m01`); cloth used to lower or lift a hot pot (`quranic:root_000248:B007/m01`); cutting meat (`quranic:root_001638:B002/m01`); exposure to cooking fire (`quranic:root_000879:B001/m01`); blazing heat (`quranic:root_000225:B001/m01`); dough baked before proper maturation (`quranic:root_001165:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:11 `مَهِّلْهُمْ`; 73:12 `جَحِيمًا`; 73:14 `ٱلْجِبَالُ`; 73:17 `يَجْعَلُ`; 73:18 `مُنفَطِرٌ`; 73:20 `ثُلُثَهُ/يُقَدِّرُ/صَّلَوٰةَ`
- Synthesis: Each motif occupies a kitchen role: supports stabilize the vessel, fire heats it, cloth protects the handler, cutting portions the ingredient, and premature baking names failed timing. The scene supplies the ordinary productive counterpart to infernal heat.

#### Subchannel B. Reducing, sweetening, and serving drink
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Natural sweets are gathered, a liquid is cooked down or blended to control strength, and the result is judged and consumed in measured sips.
- Active motifs: honey retained in its comb (`quranic:root_000822:B007/m01`); sweet gum gathered from trees (`quranic:root_001096:B007/m01`); drink reduced until one third remains (`quranic:root_000203:B008/m01`); diluting strong drink with water (`quranic:root_001200:B007/m01`); tasting and consuming (`quranic:root_000934:B001/m01`); sweetness and palatability (`quranic:root_000994:B001/m01`); a sip or breath of drinking (`quranic:root_001533:B006/m01`); oily dregs or molten residue (`quranic:root_001452:B003/m01`); cooking vessel (`quranic:root_001205:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:11 `مَهِّلْهُمْ`; 73:13 `طَعَامًا/عَذَابًا`; 73:15 `شَٰهِدًا`; 73:20 `ثُلُثَهُ/أَنفُسِكُم/يُقَدِّرُ/يُقَٰتِلُونَ/غَفُورٌ`
- Synthesis: Honey and tree gum supply sweetness, reduction concentrates, dilution softens, tasting judges, and the sip portions intake. The molten-dregs sense creates a dark bridge back to punitive heat, while palatable sweetness directly reverses the choking meal.

### 27. P2 - Prestige, Luxury, and Its Reversal
- Semantic invariant: Social distinction is constructed through comfort, rank, reputation, display, and claims of superiority, then exposed to reversal.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:11 anchors `ٱلْمُكَذِّبِينَ أُو۟لِى ٱلنَّعْمَةِ`, while 73:14-17 and 20 supply `ٱلْجِبَالُ`, `وَبِيلًا`, `شِيبًا`, `فَضْلِ`, `خَيْرًا`, and `أَعْظَمَ أَجْرًا`.
- Surprising reach: Crown, leaders-as-mountains, fame, padded bodily display, dispersal, abasement, and scarcity make luxury a fragile social construction rather than a stable possession.

#### Subchannel A. Rank, comfort, and recognized excellence
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A privileged person enjoys ease, occupies an advanced rank, and receives public recognition as excellent.
- Active motifs: blessing and good condition (`quranic:root_001525:B001/m01`); softness and luxurious living (`quranic:root_001525:B002/m01`); firstness and precedence in rank (`quranic:root_000067:B001/m01`); degree and distinction (`quranic:root_001163:B002/m01`); honor and social sanctity (`quranic:root_001029:B010/m01`); selected excellence (`quranic:root_000452:B002/m01`); beauty opposed to ugliness (`quranic:root_000323:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:11 `أُو۟لِى/ٱلنَّعْمَةِ`; 73:20 `فَضْلِ/خَيْرًا/أَعْظَمَ`
- Synthesis: Comfort, priority, distinction, honor, selection, and beauty form a social rank scene. The surface targets are not simply wealthy; they are possessors of an entire apparatus that makes privilege visible and credible.

#### Subchannel B. Display, fame, and arrogant enlargement
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Status is amplified through bodily display, crown, public fame, elite leadership, and explicit superiority claims.
- Active motifs: arrogance and self-enlargement (`quranic:root_001029:B006/m01`); padding used to enlarge bodily appearance (`quranic:root_001029:B008/m01`); crown as a covering of kingship (`quranic:root_001307:B015/m01`); good fame spreading (`quranic:root_000745:B008/m01`); public reputation (`quranic:root_000516:B007/m01`); leaders likened to mountains (`quranic:root_000217:B012/m01`); claiming superiority over peers (`quranic:root_001163:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:14 `ٱلْجِبَالُ`; 73:17 `كَفَرْتُمْ`; 73:18 `ٱلسَّمَآءُ`; 73:19 `تَذْكِرَةٌ`; 73:20 `فَضْلِ/أَعْظَمَ`
- Synthesis: Status is literally made larger, covered by a crown, broadcast as fame, and stabilized by mountain-like leaders. The scene exposes prestige as crafted display and social repetition, not intrinsic magnitude.

#### Subchannel C. Dispersal and abasement
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A privileged collective loses cohesion, status, and secure abundance under a severe event.
- Active motifs: ostrich-like dispersal and loss of collective strength (`quranic:root_001525:B008/m01`); scarcity and abasement (`quranic:root_001251:B001/m01`); others replacing or following the first (`quranic:root_000019:B001/m01`); a calamitous event (`quranic:root_001700:B003/m01`); harmful consequence (`quranic:root_001619:B002/m01`); baseness and lowered rank (`quranic:root_000493:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:11 `ٱلنَّعْمَةِ/قَلِيلًا`; 73:14,17 `يَوْمَ/يَوْمًا`; 73:16 `وَبِيلًا`; 73:20 `ءَاخَرُونَ/أَدْنَىٰ`
- Synthesis: Reversal acts socially as well as materially: the group scatters, the first gives way to others, abundance contracts, and rank falls. This process answers the apparent permanence of luxury.

### 28. P2 - Governance, Levy, and Deterrent Liability
- Semantic invariant: Rule maintains a household or polity through care, contracts, assessed transfers, testimony, and sanctions that establish responsibility.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:11, 15-16, and 20 anchor `أُو۟لِى`, `رَسُولًا شَٰهِدًا عَلَيْكُمْ`, `أَخَذْنَٰهُ`, `ءَاتُوا`, `أَقْرِضُوا`, and `أَجْرًا`.
- Surprising reach: The Pharaoh precedent activates not only kingship but a full administrative frame of household allegiance, tribute, tax, covenant, wage, surety, and exemplary punishment.

#### Subchannel A. Household rule and sustaining authority
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A ruler or household head gathers dependents, governs their affairs, and assumes responsibility for their maintenance.
- Active motifs: household and followers returning to a head (`quranic:root_000067:B003/m01`); governance and administrative repair (`quranic:root_000067:B004/m01`); ownership and sovereignty (`quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`); nurture and completion (`quranic:root_000532:B002/m01`); care and rule (`quranic:root_001273:B004/m01`); surety for another (`quranic:root_001332:B003/m01`); possessor or holder of a quality (`quranic:root_000527:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:11 `أُو۟لِى`; 73:14,18,20 `كَانَتِ/كَانَ/يَكُونُ`; 73:19-20 `رَبِّهِ/رَبَّكَ`; surface anchor unavailable for `quranic:root_000527:B001` because `ذ و و` is missing from root coverage
- Synthesis: Rule is rooted in a returning household relation, then specified as care, repair, maintenance, and surety. The possessor construction adds the status by which authority and privilege are socially attributed.

#### Subchannel B. Tribute, tax, covenant, and wage
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A polity fixes obligations through covenant and collects assessed value as tribute, tax, loan, or compensated labor.
- Active motifs: tribute or levy paid by a population (`quranic:root_000009:B008/m01`); tax imposed on person or land (`quranic:root_000906:B010/m01`); a loan or deed awaiting return (`quranic:root_001217:B003/m01`); wage and contractual hire (`quranic:root_000015:B001/m01`); covenant and protected agreement (`quranic:root_000532:B011/m01`); mutual promising (`quranic:root_001662:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:18 `وَعْدُهُ`; 73:19-20 `رَبِّهِ/رَبَّكَ/ءَاتُوا/يَضْرِبُونَ/أَقْرِضُوا/قَرْضًا/أَجْرًا`
- Synthesis: These transfers differ legally but share institutional assessment. Covenant creates the relation, tribute and tax impose payment, loan anticipates return, and wage compensates service.

#### Subchannel C. Testimony establishing sanction
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A violation is established by witness, attributed to its actor, and answered with a deterrent consequence.
- Active motifs: knowledgeable testimony (`quranic:root_000822:B002/m01`); attribution of falsehood (`quranic:root_001290:B002/m01`); accountability for an offense (`quranic:root_000018:B002/m01`); deterrent punishment (`quranic:root_001554:B003/m01`); painful sanction (`quranic:root_000994:B005/m01`); harmful consequence (`quranic:root_001619:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:11 `ٱلْمُكَذِّبِينَ`; 73:12 `أَنكَالًا`; 73:13 `عَذَابًا`; 73:15 `شَٰهِدًا`; 73:16 `أَخَذْنَٰهُ/وَبِيلًا`
- Synthesis: Witness establishes the event, attribution identifies the violation, accountability attaches it to the actor, and sanction converts judgment into deterrence. The sequence makes punishment an institutional act rather than raw force.

### 29. P2 - Mobilization, Capture, and Mortal Outcome
- Semantic invariant: Organized conflict moves from preparation and advance to close engagement, capture, subjugation, and death.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:15-16 and 20 anchor `رَسُولًا`, `أَخَذْنَٰهُ أَخْذًا وَبِيلًا`, and `يُقَٰتِلُونَ فِى سَبِيلِ ٱللَّهِ`.
- Surprising reach: The battle field includes rumor-driven mobilization, war clamor, throat holds, taming, clubs, exposure to killing, and the extinction of a group.

#### Subchannel A. Mobilization and engagement
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A force prepares, is incited, advances with courage, and meets an opposing force in reciprocal combat.
- Active motifs: preparation for war (`quranic:root_000545:B004/m01`); war at its fiercest (`quranic:root_000225:B002/m01`); incitement to fight (`quranic:root_000906:B017/m01`); reciprocal combat (`quranic:root_001200:B011/m01`); courageous forward movement (`quranic:root_001207:B006/m01`); clamor of warriors (`quranic:root_001434:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:12 `جَحِيمًا`; 73:14 `تَرْجُفُ`; 73:20 `يَضْرِبُونَ/يُقَٰتِلُونَ/تُقَدِّمُوا`; surface anchor unavailable for `quranic:root_001434:B001` because `م ع ع` is missing from root coverage
- Synthesis: Preparation and incitement precede advance; advance culminates in a two-sided encounter. Fire and clamor make mobilization audible and environmental, not only strategic.

#### Subchannel B. Capture and exposure to death
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Close engagement produces a grip, capture, bodily vulnerability, and possible extinction.
- Active motifs: arrest and captivity (`quranic:root_000018:B003/m01`); wrestling seizure (`quranic:root_000018:B011/m01`); throat grip in combat (`quranic:root_000934:B012/m01`); making someone vulnerable to killing (`quranic:root_001200:B005/m01`); extinction of a person or people (`quranic:root_001217:B009/m01`); life and soul lost at death (`quranic:root_001533:B011/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:13 `طَعَامًا`; 73:16,19 `أَخَذْنَٰهُ/أَخْذًا/ٱتَّخَذَ`; 73:20 `يُقَٰتِلُونَ/أَنفُسِكُم/قَرْضًا`
- Synthesis: Grip becomes capture, capture creates vulnerability, and vulnerability opens onto death at both individual and collective scale. The soul motif specifies what is removed when the sequence reaches its mortal outcome.

#### Subchannel C. Subjugation and the heavy instrument
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Defeated resistance is broken by force and trained into compliance.
- Active motifs: killing and removal of life (`quranic:root_001200:B001/m01`); subjugating and taming until hardness softens (`quranic:root_001200:B002/m01`); deterrent punishment (`quranic:root_001554:B003/m01`); a heavy club or beating beam (`quranic:root_001619:B004/m01`); direct striking (`quranic:root_000906:B001/m01`); transforming a person’s state (`quranic:root_000248:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:12 `أَنكَالًا`; 73:16 `أَخَذْنَٰهُ/وَبِيلًا`; 73:17 `يَجْعَلُ`; 73:20 `يَضْرِبُونَ/يُقَٰتِلُونَ`
- Synthesis: The outcome is not only death: force can also break resistance into trained compliance. Club, strike, transformation, and deterrence define a coercive post-capture process distinct from battlefield movement.

### 30. P2 - Animal Drive in Racing and Breeding
- Semantic invariant: Animal force is expressed as directed forward drive, whether toward a finish, a mate, or productive offspring.
- Surface relation: indirect; 73:15-16 and 20 anchor `أَرْسَلْنَا`, `وَبِيلًا`, `يَضْرِبُونَ`, `يَبْتَغُونَ`, `تُقَدِّمُوا`, and `تَيَسَّرَ`.
- Surprising reach: Messenger-sending, precedence, ease, deterrence, and productive increase are all pressured by a detailed equine and herd mechanics.

#### Subchannel A. Gait, racing order, and the sore hoof
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A horse is urged into smooth movement, exposes its withers, advances in race order, and may shorten stride to protect a sore hoof.
- Active motifs: smooth easy gait (`quranic:root_000563:B003/m01`); successive detachments (`quranic:root_000563:B005/m01`); the horse’s muzzle and requested run (`quranic:root_000934:B009/m01`); elevated withers beneath the rider’s hand (`quranic:root_001285:B003/m01`); a prancing horse (`quranic:root_000138:B007/m01`); a camel advancing by its foreleg gait (`quranic:root_000009:B009/m01`); courageous advance (`quranic:root_001207:B006/m01`); light responsive movement (`quranic:root_001694:B005/m01`); protecting a painful hoof (`quranic:root_001677:B003/m01`); the second horse following the winner (`quranic:root_000879:B006/m01`); an animal running and then stopping (`quranic:root_001290:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:11 `ٱلْمُكَذِّبِينَ`; 73:13 `طَعَامًا`; 73:14 `كَثِيبًا`; 73:15-16 `أَرْسَلْنَا/رَسُولًا/وَبِيلًا`; 73:17 `تَتَّقُونَ`; 73:20 `يَسَّرَ/تُقَدِّمُوا/صَّلَوٰةَ`
- Synthesis: The scene specifies body part, handling point, gait, order, motivation, and injury response. Ease becomes a quality of movement, while stopping can signal either failure or protective adaptation.

#### Subchannel B. Stallion signal, estrus, mating, and milk
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A male signals readiness, females enter estrus, mating occurs, and reproduction yields milk.
- Active motifs: stallion’s threatening roar before mounting (`quranic:root_001662:B005/m01`); female animal seeking a male (`quranic:root_000248:B009/m01`); mating of a male camel with a female (`quranic:root_000906:B011/m01`); estrus of a she-camel (`quranic:root_000009:B012/m01`); mating drive in sheep (`quranic:root_001619:B006/m01`); milk and offspring increasing (`quranic:root_001694:B006/m01`); milk flowing continuously (`quranic:root_000563:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:15-16 `أَرْسَلْنَا/ٱلرَّسُولَ/وَبِيلًا`; 73:17 `يَجْعَلُ`; 73:18 `وَعْدُهُ`; 73:20 `ءَاتُوا/يَضْرِبُونَ/تَيَسَّرَ`
- Synthesis: Signal, estrus, mating, conception potential, and lactation form a reproductive sequence. Forward drive is redirected from race progress into generative productivity.

### 31. P2 - Cutting, Shaping, and Material Finish
- Semantic invariant: Raw material becomes a finished object through cutting, striking, grinding, weaving, covering, and surface treatment.
- Surface relation: indirect; 73:14, 17-18, and 20 anchor `ٱلْجِبَالُ`, `كَفَرْتُمْ`, `مَفْعُولًا`, `يَضْرِبُونَ`, `قَرْضًا`, and `تُقَدِّمُوا`.
- Surprising reach: The language of action and fulfilled promise activates an artisan workshop containing shears, adze, mallet, grinding stone, textile, pile, coating, and tanning dose.

#### Subchannel A. The shaping workshop
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Workers cut and strike material, hew wood, grind solids, and weave a stable form.
- Active motifs: cutting with shears or teeth (`quranic:root_001217:B001/m01`); adze for shaping wood (`quranic:root_001207:B008/m01`); grinding stone (`quranic:root_000879:B008/m01`); workers including carpenter and digger (`quranic:root_001167:B003/m01`); heavy mallet or beating beam (`quranic:root_001619:B004/m01`); well-made weaving (`quranic:root_000217:B007/m01`); tools, striking place, and tent-making (`quranic:root_000906:B014/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:14 `ٱلْجِبَالُ`; 73:16 `وَبِيلًا`; 73:18 `مَفْعُولًا`; 73:20 `يَضْرِبُونَ/صَّلَوٰةَ/قَرْضًا/تُقَدِّمُوا`
- Synthesis: Cutting establishes edges, adze and mallet shape mass, grinding reduces particles, and weaving assembles strands. Worker and tool roles make this a single production floor rather than a tool list.

#### Subchannel B. Textile covering and surface treatment
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A woven surface receives nap, coating, dye, drape, or tanning treatment that changes appearance and durability.
- Active motifs: basic covering (`quranic:root_001307:B001/m01`); raised nap or hair on cloth (`quranic:root_001096:B003/m01`); draped garment (`quranic:root_001163:B005/m01`); a small tanning dose (`quranic:root_001533:B007/m01`); dyed cloth whose appearance deceives (`quranic:root_001290:B009/m01`); well-made weaving (`quranic:root_000217:B007/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:11 `ٱلْمُكَذِّبِينَ`; 73:14 `ٱلْجِبَالُ`; 73:17 `كَفَرْتُمْ`; 73:20 `فَضْلِ/أَنفُسِكُم/غَفُورٌ`
- Synthesis: Weaving creates the substrate, nap and tanning alter its physical surface, dye changes its visual claim, and draping puts the finish into use. The deceptive cloth provides a concrete analogy for covering that misrepresents what lies beneath.

### 32. P2 - Collective Assembly, Partition, and Dispersal
- Semantic invariant: A collective can gather as a whole, divide into functional portions, move in successive groups, or lose cohesion.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:11, 14-15, and 20 anchor `ٱلْمُكَذِّبِينَ`, `ٱلْجِبَالُ`, `إِلَيْكُمْ`, `طَآئِفَةٌ`, and repeated `ءَاخَرُونَ`.
- Surprising reach: Crowd, herd, party, gathered whole, successive detachment, leftward branch, and ostrich-like scattering provide a precise social morphology.

#### Subchannel A. Gathering into a whole
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Individuals gather into a crowd or party whose members are treated as one operational whole.
- Active motifs: a party or group of people (`quranic:root_000957:B004/m01`); gathering parts into one (`quranic:root_001210:B001/m01`); a multitude likened to a mountain (`quranic:root_000217:B002/m01`); large gathered groups (`quranic:root_000532:B004/m01`); a people or social body (`quranic:root_001273:B001/m01`); the whole crowd without exception (`quranic:root_001096:B008/m01`); a place filled tightly by people (`quranic:root_001091:B002/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:11 `ٱلْمُكَذِّبِينَ`; 73:13 `غُصَّةٍ`; 73:14 `ٱلْجِبَالُ`; 73:19-20 `رَبِّهِ/رَبَّكَ/طَآئِفَةٌ/تَقُومُ/غَفُورٌ/قُرْءَانِ`
- Synthesis: Gathering scales from party to people to mountain-like multitude, while crowding names the spatial consequence of density. “The whole crowd” closes the scene by removing residual outsiders.

#### Subchannel B. Portioning and scattering the collective
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A whole is divided into a party and “others,” sent in successive units, directed to a side, or dispersed in defeat.
- Active motifs: a piece or portion of a larger thing (`quranic:root_000957:B004/m02`); another group after or apart from the first (`quranic:root_000019:B001/m01`); passing a place to one side (`quranic:root_001217:B002/m01`); taking the left side (`quranic:root_001694:B004/m01`); successive groups sent one after another (`quranic:root_000563:B005/m01`); collective dispersal and loss of strength (`quranic:root_001525:B008/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:11 `ٱلنَّعْمَةِ`; 73:15-16 `أَرْسَلْنَا/ٱلرَّسُولَ`; 73:20 `طَآئِفَةٌ/ءَاخَرُونَ/قَرْضًا/تَيَسَّرَ`
- Synthesis: Division can be organized or catastrophic. Party, side, and succession produce functional units; dispersal breaks the relation that made the collective effective.

### 33. P2 - Inward Self, Desire, and Breath
- Semantic invariant: The self mediates intention, attraction, emotion, bodily life, and the need for relief.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:19-20 anchor `شَآءَ`, `لِأَنفُسِكُم`, `تَجِدُوهُ`, `ٱسْتَغْفِرُوا`, and `رَّحِيمٌ`.
- Surprising reach: “Self” extends from inward purpose to breath, blood, living soul, precious competition, grief, love, anger, and release from constriction.

#### Subchannel A. Inward purpose and affect
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: The self forms an intention, is drawn toward a valued object, and experiences love, grief, anger, or rivalry.
- Active motifs: inward purpose and discernment (`quranic:root_001533:B013/m01`); attraction of the self toward a thing (`quranic:root_000831:B005/m01`); love or grief felt inwardly (`quranic:root_001626:B004/m01`); anger and resentment (`quranic:root_001626:B005/m01`); precious value provoking competition (`quranic:root_001533:B010/m01`); the self characterized as deceptive (`quranic:root_001290:B008/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:11 `ٱلْمُكَذِّبِينَ`; 73:16,19 `أَخَذْنَٰهُ/ٱتَّخَذَ`; 73:19 `شَآءَ`; 73:20 `أَنفُسِكُم/تَجِدُوهُ`
- Synthesis: Intention supplies direction, attraction supplies motive, and affect registers the result. Competition and self-deception show how perceived value can distort that inward process.

#### Subchannel B. Breath, life, and relief
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A living body breathes, enters constriction, and receives enough opening to continue.
- Active motifs: breath leaving the body (`quranic:root_001533:B001/m01`); relief from distress (`quranic:root_001533:B002/m01`); blood as bodily life-support (`quranic:root_001533:B004/m01`); living soul (`quranic:root_001533:B011/m01`); bodily self and identity (`quranic:root_001533:B012/m01`); ease after difficulty (`quranic:root_001694:B001/m01`); mercy and tenderness (`quranic:root_000552:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:20 `تَيَسَّرَ/أَنفُسِكُم/رَّحِيمٌ`
- Synthesis: Breath, blood, soul, and bodily identity describe what must be preserved; relief and ease describe the opening that preserves it. Mercy thus enters as a life-sustaining operation, not an abstract sentiment.

### 34. P2 - Making, Existing, and Transforming
- Semantic invariant: A thing moves from possibility through creation and occurrence into a changed, identifiable state.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:14, 17-18, and 20 anchor `كَانَتِ`, `يَجْعَلُ`, `كَانَ`, `مَفْعُولًا`, `يَكُونُ`, and `تَجِدُوهُ`.
- Surprising reach: Creation, existence, fabrication, and transformation are kept distinct, allowing the fulfilled promise and altered children or mountains to be read as different ontological operations.

#### Subchannel A. Creating and bringing into existence
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An agent originates a thing, which then occurs and stands as an existing object that can be known or found.
- Active motifs: making or creating (`quranic:root_000248:B001/m01`); originating and beginning creation (`quranic:root_001165:B002/m01`); occurrence in time (`quranic:root_001332:B001/m01`); location and standing in existence (`quranic:root_001332:B002/m01`); existence after absence (`quranic:root_001626:B002/m01`); a knowable and reportable thing (`quranic:root_000831:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:14,18,20 `كَانَتِ/كَانَ/يَكُونُ`; 73:17 `يَجْعَلُ`; 73:18 `مُنفَطِرٌ`; 73:19 `شَآءَ`; 73:20 `تَجِدُوهُ`
- Synthesis: Creation names agency, occurrence supplies time, place supplies position, and existence makes the result available to knowledge and report. The stages prevent “making” from becoming an undifferentiated synonym for being.

#### Subchannel B. Beginning, fabricating, and changing state
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An agent starts an operation, may construct a false artifact, or transforms an existing object into a new condition.
- Active motifs: beginning and persisting in an act (`quranic:root_000248:B004/m01`); enacted work (`quranic:root_001167:B001/m01`); fabricated composition (`quranic:root_001167:B004/m01`); transforming a thing’s condition (`quranic:root_000248:B002/m01`); derived novelty produced from another thing (`quranic:root_001683:B005/m01`); acting hurriedly (`quranic:root_001434:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:17 `يَجْعَلُ/ٱلْوِلْدَٰنَ/شِيبًا`; 73:18 `مَفْعُولًا`; 73:20 `يَكُونُ`; surface anchor unavailable for `quranic:root_001434:B004` because `م ع ع` is missing from root coverage
- Synthesis: Beginning establishes process, work realizes it, and transformation changes an existing patient; fabrication is a rival operation that produces an artifact without truthful correspondence. Hurried work names a failure mode in which process outruns proper formation.

### 35. P2 - Capture by Device and Division by Lot
- Semantic invariant: A desired resource is acquired through an apparatus that constrains movement or assigns shares.
- Surface relation: indirect; 73:13, 17, and 20 anchor `طَعَامًا`, `تَتَّقُونَ`, `يَبْتَغُونَ`, `قَرْضًا`, and `أَنفُسِكُم`.
- Surprising reach: Hunting snare, raptor, lure, gaming arrows, quiver, carcass division, and musk-piece lot form two concrete acquisition systems.

#### Subchannel A. Hunter, lure, snare, and quarry
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A hunter draws an animal into range and captures it with a snare or trained hunting instrument.
- Active motifs: hunters going out after game (`quranic:root_000745:B006/m01`); a bow or hunting implement that feeds its owner (`quranic:root_000934:B006/m01`); a snare set for capture (`quranic:root_000879:B004/m01`); luring an animal from its den (`quranic:root_000452:B006/m01`); protective barrier around vulnerable prey (`quranic:root_001677:B001/m01`); a named raptor used in the hunting field (`quranic:root_001040:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:13 `طَعَامًا`; 73:17 `تَتَّقُونَ`; 73:18 `ٱلسَّمَآءُ`; 73:20 `خَيْرٍ/عَلِمَ/صَّلَوٰةَ`
- Synthesis: Hunter, lure, implement, snare, prey-defense, and raptor fill the roles of one capture scene. Provision here is obtained by controlling approach and escape.

#### Subchannel B. Gaming arrows and apportioned carcass
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Participants cast marked lots, use a quiver or container, divide a slaughtered animal, and assign shares.
- Active motifs: gaming arrows and division of a slaughtered camel (`quranic:root_001694:B007/m01`); the fifth gaming arrow (`quranic:root_001533:B016/m01`); a container gathering arrows (`quranic:root_000532:B010/m01`); cutting a share from a whole (`quranic:root_001217:B001/m01`); the number three as an apportioned count (`quranic:root_000203:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:17 `يَجْعَلُ`; 73:19-20 `رَبِّهِ/رَبَّكَ/ثُلُثَىِ/ثُلُثَهُ/تُحْصُوهُ/أَنفُسِكُم/قَرْضًا/تَيَسَّرَ`
- Synthesis: Marked implements randomize assignment, the container organizes them, cutting creates portions, and number identifies quantity. This is a distributive mechanism distinct from commercial valuation or charitable transfer.

### 36. P2 - Relinquishment and Transferred Handling
- Semantic invariant: Releasing an affair changes who holds, answers for, restrains, or submits it.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:11-12, 16-20 anchor `وَذَرْنِى`, `مَهِّلْهُمْ`, `أَنكَالًا`, `أَخَذْنَٰهُ`, `كَفَرْتُمْ`, `ٱتَّخَذَ`, and `تَابَ عَلَيْكُمْ`.
- Surprising reach: Leaving is not a single empty gesture: it can transfer accountability to a sovereign guarantor, recoil in weakness, impose restraint, disavow an attachment, or end in submission.

#### Subchannel A. Leaving an affair to accountable custody
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: One actor releases an affair while another assumes ownership, accountability, and surety for its handling.
- Active motifs: leaving a thing (`quranic:root_001638:B003/m01`); holding a person accountable for fault (`quranic:root_000018:B002/m01`); ownership and sovereignty (`quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`); surety and undertaking another’s care (`quranic:root_001332:B003/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:11 `وَذَرْنِى`; 73:16,19 `أَخَذْنَٰهُ/ٱتَّخَذَ`; 73:19-20 `رَبِّهِ/رَبَّكَ`; 73:14,18,20 `كَانَتِ/كَانَ/يَكُونُ`
- Synthesis: Relinquishment does not erase the affair; it moves it under a different holder. Accountability specifies judgment, sovereignty specifies authority, and surety specifies continued care, turning “leave me” into a transfer of operative control.

#### Subchannel B. Recoil, restraint, disavowal, and submission
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A participant withdraws from action through weakness or deliberate restraint, disowns a relation, or yields under pressure.
- Active motifs: recoiling from an affair through fear or weakness (`quranic:root_001554:B002/m01`); holding back and turning away (`quranic:root_000906:B004/m01`); abstaining or preventing another (`quranic:root_000994:B003/m01`); disavowal and severance from a thing (`quranic:root_001307:B005/m01`); submissive yielding (`quranic:root_001332:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:12 `أَنكَالًا`; 73:13 `عَذَابًا`; 73:17 `كَفَرْتُمْ`; 73:20 `يَضْرِبُونَ/يَكُونُ`
- Synthesis: The branches distinguish inability, chosen restraint, imposed prevention, relational disavowal, and final submission. They pressure the surface movement from reprieve to seizure by showing several non-equivalent ways agency can be withdrawn.

### 37. P2 - Bodily Signs of Crisis
- Semantic invariant: Acute crisis becomes legible through involuntary motion and visible changes in face, eye, lip, and hair.
- Surface relation: direct; 73:12, 14, 17, and 20 anchor `جَحِيمًا`, `تَرْجُفُ ٱلْأَرْضُ`, `شِيبًا`, `مَرْضَىٰ`, and `تَجِدُوهُ`.
- Surprising reach: Cosmic shaking and infernal heat are reframed as somatic diagnostics: convulsion, pulse, fear-tremor, anger-flush, jutting eye, eye-film, cleft lip, facial distortion, and greying.

#### Subchannel A. Convulsion and heated anger
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Fear or anger produces tremor, involuntary motion, pulse, and heat visible at the face.
- Active motifs: a bodily tremor or shaking (`quranic:root_000025:B008/m01`); possessed involuntary head and body movement (`quranic:root_000025:B012/m01`); disturbed pulsing or agitation (`quranic:root_000906:B012/m01`); trembling from fear or anger (`quranic:root_001251:B005/m01`); felt anger (`quranic:root_001626:B005/m01`); a face blazing with anger (`quranic:root_000225:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:11 `قَلِيلًا`; 73:12 `جَحِيمًا`; 73:14,20 `ٱلْأَرْضُ/أَرْضِ`; 73:20 `يَضْرِبُونَ/تَجِدُوهُ`
- Synthesis: Tremor supplies the gross motion, pulse the internal rhythm, and anger the affective heat that reaches the face. The earth’s convulsion and the fire’s blaze are thereby materialized as a body losing voluntary stillness.

#### Subchannel B. Face, eye, lip, and hair as diagnostic surface
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Crisis marks the body through an inflamed or jutting eye, obscured vision, a cleft lip, facial distortion, and prematurely white hair.
- Active motifs: an inflamed or jutting eye (`quranic:root_000225:B003/m01`); an eye-film like woven threads (`quranic:root_000672:B010/m01`); a visible cleft in the upper lip (`quranic:root_001040:B004/m01`); a disfigured face or form (`quranic:root_000832:B002/m01`); white hair and aging (`quranic:root_000833:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:12 `جَحِيمًا`; 73:17 `شِيبًا`; 73:19-20 `سَبِيلًا/سَبِيلِ`; 73:19 `شَآءَ`; 73:20 `عَلِمَ`
- Synthesis: These motifs treat the visible body as evidence: eye posture registers intensity, the film registers impaired perception, lip and face register altered form, and white hair registers compressed age. The child-to-grey image becomes one member of a wider diagnostic surface.

## Standalone Subchannels

### S1. P1 - Verse Modeling and Repeated Rhyme
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A composition follows an earlier poetic model and binds successive lines through repeated rhyme.
- Active motifs: a poetic method, model, or intended course (`quranic:root_001210:B011/m01`); a poem following another poem’s pattern (`quranic:root_001211:B005/m01`); repeated identical rhyme (`quranic:root_001659:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:4 `ٱلْقُرْءَانَ`; 73:6 `وَطْـًٔا`
- Synthesis: Model supplies the compositional path and rhyme supplies recurrence at its line endings. The scene usefully pressures ordered recitation by distinguishing patterned verbal making from the assembly and delivery of speech.

### S2. P1 - Treated Garment and Veil
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Hide or cloth is strengthened with a thick treatment, made into a garment or veil, and wrapped around a wearer.
- Active motifs: wrapping oneself in clothes (`quranic:root_000644:B003/m01`); a veil or covering garment (`quranic:root_001511:B006/m01`); a strong leather garment (`quranic:root_000666:B007/m01`); thick extract used to treat hide, vessels, medicine, or food (`quranic:root_000532:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:1 `ٱلْمُزَّمِّلُ`; 73:3 `نِّصْفَهُ`; 73:7 `سَبْحًا`; 73:8-9 `رَبِّكَ/رَبُّ`
- Synthesis: Treatment, material, garment form, and wrapping occupy successive roles in one clothing scene. It gives the opening epithet a material underside without merging the wearer’s covering with the distinct operation of burden-bearing.

### S3. P2 - Deictic Presentation and Response
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A speaker calls attention, presents an object for taking, asks about it, and receives a verbal response.
- Active motifs: presenting something with “take” (`quranic:root_001573:B001/m01`); attention-opening particle (`quranic:root_001573:B002/m01`); response and answering a call (`quranic:root_001573:B003/m01`); interrogative particle (`quranic:root_001573:B004/m01`); demonstrative reference (`quranic:root_000527:B003/m01`); interrogative or relative “what” (`quranic:root_000527:B004/m01`); a knowable object of reference (`quranic:root_000831:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:19 `شَآءَ`; surface anchors unavailable for the `quranic:root_001573:*` motifs because `ه ا ء` is missing from root coverage, and for the `quranic:root_000527:*` motifs because `ذ و و` is missing from root coverage
- Synthesis: The motifs form a compact exchange: attention is opened, an object is indicated and offered, a question isolates it, and a response acknowledges the call. It remains standalone because its discourse-participant structure is not the same as messenger testimony or inward volition.

### S4. P2 - Ostrich Family and Flock Dispersal
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Adult ostriches, their chicks, and ostrich-like forms gather and then scatter under pressure.
- Active motifs: adult ostrich (`quranic:root_001525:B006/m01`); objects and body-parts likened to an ostrich (`quranic:root_001525:B007/m01`); flock dispersal and loss of strength (`quranic:root_001525:B008/m01`); ostrich chick (`quranic:root_000248:B010/m01`); a newly generated being (`quranic:root_001683:B005/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:11 `ٱلنَّعْمَةِ`; 73:17 `يَجْعَلُ/ٱلْوِلْدَٰنَ`
- Synthesis: Adult, chick, and flock behavior form a complete animal scene, while analogical body forms explain how the bird’s shape enters other objects. Its characteristic scattering usefully pressures the affluent collective of 73:11 without merging with the broader social-partition channel.

### S5. P2 - Formal Text Modeling and Composition
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A verbal work selects a prior model, composes measured lines, and makes a representation that can be faithful or fabricated.
- Active motifs: a poetic method, model, or intended course (`quranic:root_001210:B011/m01`); a poem following another poem’s pattern (`quranic:root_001211:B005/m01`); composing poetry (`quranic:root_001217:B007/m01`); making a representation or illustrative example (`quranic:root_000906:B003/m01`); a form, type, or model for replication (`quranic:root_000906:B007/m01`); fabricated composition without a prior truthful model (`quranic:root_001167:B004/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:18 `مَفْعُولًا`; 73:20 `ٱقْرَءُوا/قُرْءَانِ/قَرْضًا/يَضْرِبُونَ`
- Synthesis: Pattern, poetic making, representation, and type describe formal construction; fabrication identifies its truth-failing counterpart. This remains separate from textual access because it concerns how a verbal object is modeled and made, not how much of it can be read.

### S6. Whole Surah - Night Patrol and Road Guardianship
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A watchman repeatedly circles at night, guarding a route used by travelers.
- Active motifs: circling around a place (`quranic:root_000957:B001/m01`); a night watchman making protective rounds (`quranic:root_000957:B005/m01`); nighttime movement (`quranic:root_001392:B002/m01`); road users and travelers (`quranic:root_000672:B002/m01`); a sufficient guardian and guarantor (`quranic:root_001681:B006/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:2,6,20 `ٱلَّيْلَ/ٱلَّيْلِ`; 73:9 `وَكِيلًا`; 73:19-20 `سَبِيلًا/سَبِيلِ`; 73:20 `طَآئِفَةٌ`
- Synthesis: Route, traveler, darkness, repeated circuit, and guardian form a complete patrol scene. It materializes night standing as wakeful vigilance and usefully reframes `وَكِيلًا` as active protection exercised through repeated rounds rather than static custody.

### S7. Whole Surah - Lashed Raft and River Crossing
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Inflated skins or timbers are bound into a floating carrier, loaded, and used to continue a route across a river.
- Active motifs: a thick ship rope or hawser (`quranic:root_000260:B002/m01`); a raft of inflated skins or lashed wood (`quranic:root_000957:B006/m01`); lifting and carrying a load (`quranic:root_001251:B004/m01`); a river channel (`quranic:root_001559:B001/m01`); a road or means of passage (`quranic:root_000672:B001/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:2-3,11 `قَلِيلًا`; 73:7,20 `ٱلنَّهَارِ`; 73:10 `جَمِيلًا`; 73:19-20 `سَبِيلًا/سَبِيلِ`; 73:20 `طَآئِفَةٌ`
- Synthesis: Rope supplies cohesion, the raft supplies buoyancy, and the river supplies the interrupted route that the loaded craft restores. This materializes path-taking and livelihood travel as engineered passage, while showing how binding can turn unstable water from barrier into support.

### S8. Whole Surah - Practice Archery and the Stray Arrow
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An archer handles a bow, trains at close range with a short unpointed arrow, and confronts the danger of a shot whose source or direction is unknown.
- Active motifs: the curved working section of a bow (`quranic:root_000957:B008/m01`); a short arrow (`quranic:root_000563:B011/m01`); a small unpointed, featherless practice arrow (`quranic:root_001285:B004/m01`); close range that enables a hit (`quranic:root_001285:B002/m01`); a stray arrow of unknown origin (`quranic:root_001077:B013/m01`)
- Ayah anchors: 73:9 `ٱلْمَغْرِبِ`; 73:14 `كَثِيبًا`; 73:15-16 `أَرْسَلْنَا/رَسُولًا/ٱلرَّسُولَ`; 73:20 `طَآئِفَةٌ`
- Synthesis: Bow, training projectile, distance, successful aim, and uncontrolled shot occupy distinct roles in one range scene. It materializes the surah’s casting and sending of speech as directed delivery, while the anonymous stray arrow usefully pressures hostile or irresponsible saying as force detached from accountable origin.

### S9. Whole Surah - Milking, Aging, and Thickening a Drink
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Milk is expressed, retained in a vessel, allowed to mature or thicken, blended, reduced, diluted, and tasted.
